No new voting method is proposed; code and evidence are committed and reproducible.

</details>


### [139] [Do Large Language Models Hallucinate Electric Fata Morganas?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18816)
*Kristina Šekrst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: AI hallucinations - that is, outputs which are made up, cannot be verified, or contradict the source material - are generally regarded as an engineering flaw to be dealt with. This paper contends that they also have philosophical significance when it comes to the question of machine consciousness. We examine the known causes of hallucinations in large language models - such as source-target divergence, discrepancies between training and inference, and overfitting - and we present two empirical investigations. In the first, we apply successive generations of the GPT model to ambiguous factual questions under different temperature settings, finding that higher temperatures result in plausible but incorrect answers while lower temperatures lead to factually accurate ones. The sampling parameters that cause a model to seem creative or spontaneous and thus more likely to pass behavioral tests of intelligence are the same ones that increase its hallucination rate. In the second, we look at an encoder-only model that has been trained on encyclopedic data and which answers questions of the same type factually and without embellishment, indicating that hallucinations are due to exposure to subjective and socially diverse training data rather than to the development of any cognitive ability. Using references to Turing, Searle's Chinese Room, the frame problem, and the cybernetic tradition of Wiener and Ashby, we claim that a model's self-reports of emotion or sentience come within the definition of hallucination, and that any future occurrence of machine consciousness might remain epistemically inaccessible since it would be indistinguishable from a sufficiently advanced hallucination.

</details>


### [140] [Identifying Implicit Premises for Logical Reconstruction of Argument Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18821)
*Xuyao Feng,Anthony Hunter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对自然语言论证中普遍存在的省略前提问题，提出一种神经符号管道：先用大语言模型生成隐含前提并转为逻辑公式，再与显式前提/主张的逻辑公式结合，通过逻辑推理判断语句间的蕴含、矛盾或中立关系，并在专用语料库上完成实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言论证中的省略前提（enthymeme）普遍存在，导致论证图的重建困难。现有 NLP 方法可识别省略前提，符号方法可通过溯因补充缺失前提，但缺乏能够直接生成隐含前提以显式展示语句间逻辑关系（蕴含/矛盾）的方法。

Method: 采用神经符号混合方法：首先用大语言模型（LLM）从自然语言中推断并生成隐含前提，然后将这些前提转换为逻辑公式；接着结合显式前提和主张的逻辑公式，通过逻辑推理判断三者之间的逻辑关系（蕴含、矛盾或中立）。

Result: 所提方法在 Microtext Argumentative Corpus 上进行了评估，验证了利用 LLM 生成隐含前提并结合逻辑推理来判断语句间逻辑关系（蕴含、矛盾、中立）的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种神经符号管道，利用大语言模型生成隐含前提并将其转换为逻辑公式，结合显式前提与主张的逻辑公式，实现自然语言论证中逻辑关系的自动化重建，并在 Microtext 论证语料库上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: The logical reconstruction of argument graphs from natural language text is challenging because of the prevalence of enthymemes (i.e., arguments with implicit premises). There are natural language processing methods for identifying enthymemes in text, and there are symbolic methods based on abduction for identifying missing premises in a logical representation of enthymemes. However, there is a need for methods to generate implicit premises to logically show a known entailment or contradiction relationship between a pair of statements. To address this, we propose a neuro-symbolic pipeline that uses large language models (LLMs) to generate intermediate implicit premises that are translated into logical formulae and used with logical formulae representing explicit premises and explicit claims to show the logical relationships between them (entailment, contradiction, or neutrality). Our approach is evaluated on the Microtext Argumentative Corpus.

</details>


### [141] [Understanding Multilingual Medical ASR Adaptation Through Layer-Wise Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18825)
*Souranil Kahali,Rituparna Bose,Abner Hernandez,Tomas Arias-Vergara,Andreas Maier,Ning Ma,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过层状编码器分析，探究多语言医学适配如何重塑Whisper模型的内部表示，比较不同微调策略的效果，发现英语医学微调对编码器表示影响最大，多语言延续则保持已适配表示空间，且随着词错率改善，可线性恢复的错误预测线索减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学自动语音识别（MedASR）需要适应专业术语、有限的标注临床数据和多语言用例。虽然大规模预训练ASR模型如Whisper泛化能力强，但其在医学和多语言适配后的行为尚未充分理解，仅限于词错率（WER）指标。因此需要研究适配对模型内部表示的影响。

Method: 采用层状编码器分析方法，比较零样本解码、仅英语微调、仅德语诊断微调、两阶段EN->EN+DE延续和直接EN+DE微调等策略，跨不同Whisper模型规模。重点分析两阶段Whisper-Small的轨迹。

Result: 微调显著提升MedASR性能，但最佳模型取决于适配设置：Whisper-Medium在直接EN+DE训练下取得最低英语WER（7.72%）和最低EN+DE组合WER（26.30%）；仅德语Whisper-Large-v3取得最低德语WER（44.96%），但基于86条单说话人训练话语的语料内诊断，而非稳健泛化。层状分析显示英语医学微调产生主导编码器变化，多语言延续基本保持已适配表示空间；领域和语言信息在各层高度可恢复，而可线性恢复的错误预测线索随WER改善而减弱。

Conclusion: 多语言医学适配对Whisper模型内部表示的塑造具有特定规律：英语医学微调起主导作用，多语言延续则保留现有表示；适配后模型性能提升但依赖具体设置，且错误预测线索的线性可恢复性随性能改善而下降。

Abstract: Medical automatic speech recognition (MedASR) requires adaptation to specialised terminology, limited annotated clinical data, and multilingual use cases. Although large-scale pretrained ASR models such as Whisper achieve strong generalisation, their behaviour after medical and multilingual adaptation remains insufficiently understood beyond word error rate (WER). This paper investigates how multilingual medical adaptation reshapes the internal representations of Whisper models through layer-wise encoder analysis. We compare zero-shot decoding, English-only fine-tuning, German-only diagnostic fine-tuning, two-stage EN->EN+DE continuation, and direct EN+DE fine-tuning across Whisper model sizes. Fine-tuning substantially improves MedASR performance, but the best model depends on the adaptation setting: Whisper-Medium gives the lowest English WER (7.72%) and the lowest combined EN+DE WER under direct EN+DE training (26.30%); German-only Whisper-Large-v3 gives the lowest German WER (44.96%), but as a within-corpus diagnostic on 86 single-speaker training utterances rather than robust generalisation. Layer-wise analysis of the two-stage Whisper-Small trajectory shows that English medical fine-tuning produces the dominant encoder shift, whereas multilingual continuation largely preserves the adapted representation space. Domain and language information remain highly recoverable across layers, while linearly recoverable error-predictive cues weaken as WER improves.

</details>


### [142] [Assessing Quality of Experience in Natural Language Generation of German Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18888)
*Dinh Nam Pham,Shushen Manakhimova,Vivien Macketanz,Sebastian Möller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TextQ-German数据集套件，从用户体验质量角度对德语NLG系统进行人机协作评估，开发了QoE预测模型，其中混合模型表现最优，为NLG评估提供了公开资源和基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自然语言生成技术的快速发展，特别是大型语言模型的广泛应用，可靠地评估生成文本变得日益重要。传统自动指标无法捕捉感知质量的多面性特征，需要开发新的评估方法和数据集来更好地衡量生成文本的实际用户体验质量。

Method: 通过众包研究与德语母语者合作，收集人类质量评分并识别相关感知质量维度；开发包括基于Transformer、基于语言学特征和混合方法的自动QoE预测模型；将数据集扩展为包含标注了整体QoE分数的LLM生成输出。

Result: 混合模型在几乎所有实验设置中均优于纯Transformer基线；仅使用语言学特征就能接近微调语言模型的性能；在保留集上的最终验证表明模型对未见数据具有良好的泛化能力；数据集涵盖了自动文本摘要和机器翻译任务。

Conclusion: 这项工作贡献了一个公开可访问的NLG评估资源和自动QoE预测基线，为开发更好地与人类质量感知对齐的NLG系统奠定了基础，推动从用户体验质量角度评估生成文本的研究进展。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Natural Language Generation (NLG) has made the reliable evaluation of generated text increasingly critical, as these systems, such as large language models (LLMs), are now widely deployed in real-world applications. However, traditional automatic metrics fail to capture the multifaceted nature of perceived quality. In this paper, we introduce TextQ-German, a novel dataset suite for human-centered evaluation of German NLG from a Quality of Experience (QoE) perspective, covering automatic text summarization and machine translation. Through crowdsourcing studies with German speakers, we collect human quality ratings and identify relevant perceptual quality dimensions for each task. We develop automatic QoE prediction models, including transformer-based, linguistic feature-based, and hybrid approaches. Hybrid models outperform pure transformer baselines in almost all experimental settings, while linguistic features alone can approach the performance of fine-tuned language models. The dataset is extended with LLM-generated outputs annotated with overall QoE scores. Final validation on held-out sets indicates generalization to unseen data. Our work contributes a publicly accessible resource for NLG evaluation and baselines for automatic QoE prediction, providing a foundation for developing NLG systems that better align with human quality perception.

</details>


### [143] [SMTrap: Cost-Effective DoS Attacks Against Large Reasoning Models via SMT Conflict Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18921)
*Jian Yang,Zhenqi Feng,Zhaoyang Yu,Zhaoxin Fan,Kejian Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Jianjun Huang,Wei You,Bin Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"搜索放大"(search amplification)——一种无需模型反馈的LRM-DoS攻击范式。利用SMT求解器的冲突计数作为低成本外部信号，在仅CPU环境下生成计算密集的约束满足问题(CSP)查询，实现比现有方法强数倍的拒绝服务攻击效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LRM-DoS方法严重依赖模型反馈来合成攻击查询，需要反复查询目标模型或训练专用攻击模型。这些昂贵的操作严重削弱了攻击的性价比和实用性。

Method: 提出搜索放大范式：1) 关键洞察——LRM在求解CSP时使用试错回溯搜索，SMT冲突计数与LRM回溯搜索范围和输出长度呈正相关；2) 利用SMT求解器冲突计数作为低成本外部信号；3) 构建SMTrap框架——轻量级、仅CPU运行、无需模型查询/攻击模型训练/GPU计算。

Result: 在七个前沿模型上的评估显示SMTrap达到最先进的LRM-DoS能力，产生的DoS效果比现有基线强数倍。同时演示了一种基于工具的缓解方案，可显著降低token使用量。

Conclusion: SMTrap成功实现了无需模型反馈的高效LRM-DoS攻击，通过SMT冲突计数指导CSP生成，在资源受限环境下达到最先进攻击效果。研究同时指出了潜在的缓解方向。

Abstract: Existing LRM-DoS methods rely heavily on model feedback to synthesize attack queries, requiring either repeated queries to the target model or training a dedicated attack model. These expensive operations severely weaken attack leverage. In this paper, we propose \emph{search amplification}, a novel, model-feedback-free LRM-DoS paradigm. It employs the conflict count derived from an Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT) solver as a low-cost external signal to guide the synthesis of inference-heavy Constraint Satisfaction Problem (CSP) instances. Our key observation is that LRMs depend on trial-and-backtracking search when solving CSPs, where higher SMT conflict counts on a given CSP instance positively correlate with more extensive LRM backtracking search and substantially longer output trajectories. Building on this finding, we propose \textsc{SMTrap}, a lightweight, CPU-only framework. Guided by SMT conflict counts, \textsc{SMTrap} generates inference-heavy CSP queries without model queries, attack-model training, or GPU computation. Evaluations across seven frontier models demonstrate the state-of-the-art LRM-DoS capability of \textsc{SMTrap}, producing DoS effects multiple times stronger than existing baselines. To mitigate the threat of \textsc{SMTrap}, we demonstrate a tool-based mitigation that significantly cuts token usage.

</details>


### [144] [Test-Time Scaling in the Wild: Why Exploitation, Not Exploration, Is the Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18931)
*Davide Romano,Kanak Raj,Jerrod Parker,Daniele Giofrè*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了语言模型的测试时缩放（TTS）方法在开放式生成任务上的表现，发现虽然探索（生成多个候选）随计算量增加而稳步提升，但利用（选择最佳候选）受到奖励模型质量差的严重影响，导致选择过程近乎随机。只有候选合成（Fusion）能一致地改善结果，但仍仅恢复了40%的可用质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTS技术主要在有简单验证的任务（如数学和代码）上开发和测试，但其在开放式生成任务（医学、法律、金融、通用聊天和创意写作）上的效果尚不明确。作者旨在提供跨多样基准的计算归一化比较。

Method: 对五个TTS家族（生成、搜索、精炼、融合等）在五个开放式生成基准上进行了计算归一化比较，使用统一框架将每种方法的token预算有效性分解为探索和利用两个组件，并分析各TTS方法在这两个维度的表现。

Result: 扩大探索有效：最佳候选质量随计算量稳步提升。利用失败：奖励模型与真实质量相关性差（ρ_v≈0.12），使选择近乎随机。树搜索因多样性崩溃而失败。精炼仅在一个基准上有效，其他改善被混淆因素干扰。只有融合能一致改善，但仅恢复约40%可用质量。

Conclusion: 候选池不是瓶颈，选择才是。开放式生成中TTS的主要限制是用于选择的奖励模型质量差，而非候选生成本身。建议未来工作重点应放在改善利用机制（如更好的奖励模型或选择算法）上，而非仅仅扩大探索。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) improves language model outputs by spending additional inference compute - generating multiple candidates, searching over partial sequences, or iteratively refining drafts. These techniques yield large gains on mathematics and code, but have been developed and stress-tested almost exclusively on tasks where verification is straightforward. We conduct the first compute-normalised comparison of five TTS families across five open-ended generation benchmarks spanning medicine, law, finance, general chat, and creative writing - grounded in a unified framework that decomposes the effectiveness of each method's token budget into exploration and exploitation. The answer depends on which side of that decomposition you examine. Scaling exploration works: the best candidate in the pool improves steadily with compute across all settings. What breaks is exploitation - the step that converts a rich candidate pool into a final output. With state-of-the-art generators, reward models correlate at only $ρ_v \approx 0.12$ with true quality, rendering selection near-random regardless of budget. Tree search amplifies this failure through diversity collapse. Refinement helps on one of five benchmarks; its apparent gains elsewhere are confounded. Only synthesis across candidates (Fusion) consistently improves over single-sample baselines, yet still recovers only ~40% of available quality. The candidate pool is not the bottleneck - choosing from it is.

</details>


### [145] [MedUAG: Unified Understanding and Generation for Medical Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18937)
*Zijie Meng,Yuncheng Zhang,Hualiang Wang,Yitian Tang,Xiaotang Gai,Chen Shen,Songtao Jiang,Shaosheng Cao,Jian Wu,Xian Wu,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了医疗统一理解与生成(UAG)的基础设施，包括最大规模医疗UAG数据集MedUAGCorpus、系统性评测基准MedUAGBench，以及端到端训练的医疗UAG模型MedUAG，为下一代医疗多模态系统奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)正快速发展为统一理解与生成(UAG)框架，但将其扩展至医疗领域面临两大障碍：缺乏全面的训练和评测基准，以及缺乏经过广泛验证的统一医疗模型。

Method: 1) 构建MedUAGCorpus：迄今最大的统一医疗理解与生成数据集，包含超过600万实例，涵盖14种影像模态；2) 提出MedUAGBench：系统性评测基准，在标准化协议下将医疗生成评测扩展至12种多样化任务；3) 基于上述资源开发MedUAG：端到端训练的 unified 医疗模型。

Result: 大量实验表明，MedUAG在广泛的理解决与生成任务中均展现出强劲性能，确立了有竞争力的基线。

Conclusion: 本文为医疗UAG建立了竞争基线，为下一代医疗多模态系统铺平了道路，推动医疗领域多模态AI的发展。

Abstract: Recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are rapidly evolving into unified understanding and generation (UAG) frameworks. However, extending these unified paradigms to the medical domain is hindered by: the absence of comprehensive training and evaluation benchmarks, and the lack of broadly validated unified medical model. To address these gaps, we present a comprehensive foundation for medical UAG. First, we construct MedUAGCorpus, the largest unified medical understanding and generation dataset to date, comprising over 6 million instances across 14 imaging modalities. Second, we introduce MedUAGBench, a systematic benchmark that expands medical generation evaluation to 12 diverse tasks under standardized protocols. Finally, leveraging these resources, we develop MedUAG, an end-to-end trained unified medical model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MedUAG achieves strong performance across a wide array of understanding and generation tasks, establishing a competitive baseline and paving the way for next-generation medical multimodal systems.

</details>


### [146] [Institutional Newspapers Pipeline: Deriving billions of high quality tokens from historical newspapers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18972)
*Matteo Cargnelutti,Catherine Brobston,Eben English,Jake Sadow,Kacie Bailey,Greg Leppert,Amanda Watson,Jessica Chapel,Jonathan Zittrain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个与波士顿公共图书馆联合设计的模块化新闻报纸处理流水线系统，能够从历史报纸扫描图像中提取高质量结构化数据集。该系统将扫描图分割为独立区域，经过OCR、文本分析、分类、实体识别等多个处理步骤，最终从147万份1795-1930年间出版的公共领域报纸中提取了163亿token的文本数据，并作为开放数据集发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史报纸是公众生活的丰富记录，但其密集、不规则且有时含噪声的版面布局使得对这些材料的计算访问既具有挑战性又受到限制。需要一种能够高效、准确地从大量历史报纸扫描图像中提取结构化数据的方法。

Method: 提出"机构报纸流水线"（Institutional Newspapers Pipeline），这是一个模块化系统，具有以下特点：1）每个步骤都可解释且可定制；2）计算资源消耗低，可在工作站级别硬件上运行。处理流程包括：扫描图像分割为独立区域→OCR识别→文本分析→类型分类→阅读顺序检测→命名实体识别→主题分类→语言检测→预计算嵌入生成。

Result: 在波士顿公共图书馆馆藏的一部分上运行该流水线，发布了开放数据集。OCR输出包含163亿token，跨越8310万个独立区域，从147万份公共领域报纸扫描中提取，涵盖1795-1930年间的出版物。同时发布了流水线、模型和数据集。

Conclusion: 这项工作是解锁数千万份报纸扫描图像中高质量数据的重要一步。报告详细描述各处理步骤的方法、训练的轻量级模型、评估结果和收集的数据集规模测量，并伴随流水线、模型和数据集的发布，为历史报纸的数字化和计算访问提供了重要的工具和资源。

Abstract: Historical newspapers are an abundant record of public life, but their dense, irregular and sometimes noisy layouts make computational access to these materials both challenging and limited. We present the Institutional Newspapers Pipeline, a modular system we jointly designed with Boston Public Library to extract high-quality, structured datasets from historical newspaper scans. It was architected so that each step remains interpretable and customizable, and so that the pipeline as a whole remains computationally frugal enough to run on workstation-level hardware. The pipeline runs each scan through a multi-step process: it segments scans into individual type-agnostic crops and performs OCR on each resulting segment before then performing text analysis, type classification, reading order detection, named entities recognition, subject classification, language detection, and pre-computed embeddings generation on every crop. We ran this pipeline against a portion of Boston Public Library's holdings and released the results as an open dataset. The optical character recognition (OCR) output represents 16.3 billion o200k_base tokens across 83.1 million individual crops, extracted from 1,473,635 public domain newspaper scans published between 1795 and 1930. This report describes our methods for each processing step, the small models we trained, as well as the evaluation results and dataset-scale measurements we collected in the process. It accompanies the release of the pipeline, models, and dataset. We position this work as a substantial step towards unlocking high-quality data from tens of millions of newspaper scans.

</details>


### [147] [DeepWeaver: Bridging the Evidence Synthesis Gap in Open-Ended Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18988)
*Xujia Wang,Yizhe Zhang,Bin Xu,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DeepWeaver是一个新型框架，通过维护Thought Block Chains（TBCs）结构化表示，将检索到的嘈杂证据编织成全面且有准确引用的答案，有效桥接了检索与生成之间的证据综合差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索-生成流水线用于回答开放式问题时，单纯检索不足以产生高质量答案。LLMs需要将嘈杂、碎片化的证据组织成全面的、有准确引用的答案，这一过程称为证据综合。然而，直接生成方法往往存在证据利用不足、引用错位、信息过度压缩为浅层摘要等问题，暴露出检索与生成之间的证据综合差距。

Method: DeepWeaver框架维护Thought Block Chains（TBCs），这是一种结构化表示方法，将主张、关键信息、关键词和支持证据分组管理。框架使用从属TBCs执行三项关键操作：检查残余证据、提交TBC修订、发现新主张，最后进行答案生成。该方法通过多层级结构化证据管理，实现深度信息整合。

Result: 在知识库和网页开放式QA任务上评估DeepWeaver，并引入LoQA高密度证据综合基准。实验表明，跨多种LLMs，DeepWeaver在LoQA上显著提升了内容充分性、引用准确性和细节保持能力；在DeepResearch Bench上实现了更深入的洞察和更高的引用质量。

Conclusion: 证据编织（evidence weaving）是桥接开放式QA中检索与生成环节的有效机制。DeepWeaver通过TBCs结构化表示实现了深度证据综合，验证了该方法在提升答案质量方面的有效性。

Abstract: Retrieve-then-generate pipelines are commonly used to produce deep-research answers for open-ended questions, but retrieval alone is insufficient: LLMs must organize noisy and fragmented evidence into comprehensive, well-cited answers. We refer to this process as evidence synthesis. However, direct generation often underuses evidence, misaligns citations, and collapses diverse information into shallow summaries, exposing an evidence synthesis gap between retrieval and generation. Thus, we propose DeepWeaver, a novel framework that weaves noisy retrieved evidence into comprehensive answers by maintaining Thought Block Chains (TBCs), a structured representation that groups claims, salient information, keywords, and supporting evidence. DeepWeaver uses subordinate TBCs to inspect residual evidence, commit TBC revisions, and discover new claims before final generation. We evaluate DeepWeaver on open-ended QA over both knowledge bases and the web, and introduce LoQA, a high-density benchmark for evidence synthesis. Across multiple LLMs, DeepWeaver improves content sufficiency, citation grounding, and detail preservation on LoQA, while achieving deeper insights and higher citation quality on DeepResearch Bench. These results show that evidence weaving is an effective mechanism for bridging retrieval and generation in open-ended QA. Our code is available at https://github.com/KlozeWang/DeepWeaver.

</details>


### [148] [Structure, Association, and Decision Value: Representation-Based Difficulty Estimation for Adaptive Inference in African-Language NLI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19003)
*Toheeb Ogunade*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在多语言非洲NLP中，内部表示统计量能否为自适应推理提供有效的样例级难度信号。通过对15种非洲语言的天然语言推理任务研究发现：AfriXNLI英语配置与XNLI测试数据高度重叠；参数数量不能可靠地反映跨语言性能差异；角离散度比有效秩更具语言依赖性；不同统计量对不同类型的计算收益预测效果不同。在测试条件下，没有已评估信号能证明自适应路由优于始终使用昂贵推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在探讨在多语言非洲NLP场景下，内部表示统计量是否能提供有用的样例级难度信号，以支持自适应推理策略。这一研究动机源于多语言NLP领域中自适应推理策略的有效性尚不明确，特别是在非洲语言这一相对缺乏资源的场景下。

Method: 研究团队在15种非洲语言上开展天然语言推理(NLI)实验，使用冻结的预训练检查点进行评测。他们分析了多种内部表示统计量，包括角离散度和有效秩，并评估了不同模型配置下的自适应推理性能。特别关注AfriXNLI数据集的数据重叠问题以及跨语言模型性能比较。

Result: 研究获得四项主要结果：1) AfriXNLI英语配置与XNLI评测数据高度重叠(1047/1050例完全相同)，表明存在测试集暴露问题；2) 参数数量不能可靠地排序跨语言能力，大模型在7种语言上表现更好，在8种语言上表现更差；3) 在三种多语言表示空间中，角离散度始终比有效秩更具语言依赖性；4) 有效统计关联取决于目标变量：有效秩能预测升级带来的概率增益但不能预测预测是否改变，而廉价模型置信度呈现相反模式。在测试条件下，没有已评估信号使自适应路由优于始终使用昂贵推理，但理想化的oracle策略在60%计算预算下仍比始终昂贵推理高出11个准确率点。

Conclusion: 作者的核心方法论发现是：一个表示统计量可能对一种计算收益概念具有统计显著性，但对另一种计算收益概念却无关紧要，因此是一个糟糕的决策变量。在测试的模型、信号和计算预算下，没有已评估信号证明自适应路由策略优于始终使用昂贵推理策略，这表明当前自适应推理方法在多语言非洲NLP场景下仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: We ask whether internal representation statistics can provide useful example-level difficulty signals for adaptive inference in multilingual African NLP, and find that they cannot in this setting. Studying natural language inference across 15 African languages with frozen off-the-shelf checkpoints, we report four results. First, AfriXNLI's English configuration shares 1,047 of its 1,050 examples verbatim with XNLI evaluation data, and one widely used NLI checkpoint scores 1.000 on that test split, consistent with XNLI test exposure. Because AfriXNLI is derived from XNLI, its English, French and Swahili configurations cannot serve as clean evaluations for XNLI-trained models. Second, parameter count does not reliably order capability across African languages: our larger checkpoint is better in seven languages and worse in eight, with no significant aggregate difference. Third, across three multilingual representation spaces, angular dispersion is consistently more language-determined than effective rank, so pooled correlations can inflate one and mask the other. Fourth, the association that survives language control depends on the target: effective rank predicts probability gain from escalation but not whether escalation changes the prediction, while cheap-model confidence shows the opposite pattern; the two targets correlate at only 0.655. Under the tested models, signals, and compute budgets, no evaluated signal makes adaptive routing preferable to always-expensive inference, although an oracle exceeds it by 11 accuracy points at 60% of the compute. Our central methodological finding is that a representation statistic can be statistically significant for one notion of computational benefit while being irrelevant to another, and therefore be a poor decision variable.

</details>


### [149] [Introducing the Privacy-HSD Trade-off: Hate Speech Detection, but not at the Cost of Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19006)
*Stephen Meisenbacher,Vlad Garbuz,Chirill Donos,Maxim Dnestreanschii,Gabriel Creanga,Andreea-Elena Bodea,Thomas Lampert,Jana Diesner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 仇恨言论检测（HSD）与用户隐私之间存在潜在冲突。研究通过评估多种文本脱敏方法（包括新提出的AgnoSpeech技术），验证了在保持检测性能的同时保护隐私是困难但可行的，呼吁进一步研究隐私与HSD的平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论对在线用户（尤其是青少年和少数群体）构成严重威胁，自动检测系统（HSD）的建设至关重要，但必须在技术上实现与个人隐私权的平衡。研究动机在于揭示HSD系统可能无意中通过编码作者信息而损害隐私，从而提出需要系统性权衡隐私与检测效果的必要性。

Method: 研究通过探索HSD与隐私的交叉点，建立“隐私-HSD权衡”概念，并基准测试一系列文本脱敏方法，同时引入领域特定的AgnoSpeech技术。方法核心在于通过实验验证隐私保护技术对HSD性能的影响，并分析两者间的可调和性。

Result: 实验结果表明，平衡隐私与HSD存在显著难度，但通过合理的脱敏技术（如AgnoSpeech）可以实现可行性。研究发现当前HSD系统可能隐含隐私风险，需通过技术手段优化权衡关系。

Conclusion: 研究强有力地论证了隐私与仇恨言论检测之间的权衡需要更多深入研究，两者对在线参与的保障具有实际影响。结论呼吁在技术开发中系统性考虑隐私保护，以实现安全与权利的共存。

Abstract: Hate speech is a real and timely threat that affects a large portion of online users, especially youth and minority groups. While building reliable and robust automatic hate speech detection (HSD) systems is paramount, we argue that this must also be balanced with the individual right to privacy. Exploring the intersection of HSD and privacy, we demonstrate that HSD systems might unintentionally achieve performance at the cost of encoding authorship, posing a threat to privacy. Building on these findings, we establish the notion of a privacy-HSD trade-off, which demands a careful balance. We benchmark a series of text privatization methods, as well as our newly proposed domain-specific AgnoSpeech technique, showing that balancing privacy and HSD is difficult but feasible. The findings make a strong case for more research on the trade-offs between privacy and HSD, both of which have tangible implications for the safeguarding of online participation.

</details>


### [150] [Grading the Graders: Verification Autonomy Levels (L0-L5) for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19009)
*Yajie Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出验证自主级别(VAL)框架，统一大语言模型验证器的分类标准。VAL沿单一轴——验证规范的来源和判决的保证内容——将验证方案分为L0(模型自声明)到L5(不可达)六个级别，解决了现有文献中"级别"一词的多义性混淆问题，并识别出完备性盲区：基于替换和采样的验证器只能确认候选项的有效性，无法证明没有遗漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型验证器领域的"验证级别"概念存在严重混淆，"level"一词至少被用来表示五种不同含义：验证粒度、概念抽象、风险层级、系统栈层级和事实真相的唯识论来源。这种术语不一致导致了跨论文的混淆和比较困难，需要一个统一的元标准来厘清概念。

Method: 作者提出验证自主级别(VAL)框架，沿单一轴(验证规范的来源和判决的保证内容)对验证方案进行分类。该研究在四个领域(符号数学、行为监控、医疗诊断和代码生成)进行文档化，并分析现有最强形式验证基线，同时调查17篇论文以识别术语混淆模式。

Result: VAL框架将验证分为L0(无确定性锚点的LLM自声明)到L5(无限情况下不可达)六个级别，L2对应客观事实真相(仅正确性)，L3/L4对应具有单属性或领域级完备性的可判定系统。研究发现完备性仅对形式化可规范属性可达，而经验性开放世界验证(事实检查、诊断)最高只能达到L2(锚定正确性)。验证粒度、概念层次、风险和系统栈与VAL正交，解决了17篇论文中的系统性混淆。

Conclusion: VAL框架通过明确定义验证规范的来源和判决保证内容，为大语言模型验证提供了统一的分类标准，解决了现有文献中的术语混淆问题。研究发现存在完备性盲区：基于替换和采样的验证方法无法证明完备性，只能保证正确性。该框架为正交验证维度(粒度、概念层次、风险、系统栈)与VAL的区分提供了理论基础，相关代码和完整评估作为补充材料发布。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly paired with verifiers (step checkers, self-consistency filters, tool-based fact checkers, formal proof assistants) that claim to detect the model's errors. Yet the verification literature uses the word "level" to mean at least five different things: verification granularity, concept abstraction, risk tier, system-stack layer, and the epistemic source of the ground truth. We propose Verification Autonomy Levels (VAL), a meta-standard classifying verification schemes along a single axis: where does the verification spec come from, and what does the verdict guarantee? VAL ranges from L0 (LLM self-declaration, no deterministic anchor) through L2 (objective ground truth, correctness only) to L3/L4 (decidable systems with single-property or domain-level completeness), with L5 impossible in the unrestricted case. Central to VAL is the completeness blind spot: substitution- and sampling-based verifiers can confirm that proposed candidates hold, but cannot prove that no candidate was missed. We further identify a dichotomy the literature has not stated: completeness is reachable only for formally specifiable properties, while empirical open-world verification (fact-checking, diagnosis) caps at anchored correctness (L2). We document this across four domains (symbolic mathematics, behavior monitoring, medical diagnosis, and code generation) and in the strongest existing formal-verification baseline, whose authors note the verifier "focuses on the correctness of each step." We show the levels of granularity, concept hierarchy, risk, and system stack are orthogonal to VAL, resolving a systematic conflation across 17 surveyed papers. Code and full assessment are released as supplementary material.

</details>


### [151] [Institutional Books - Enriched Text: A customizable multilingual open-source pipeline for denoising, deduplicating, and annotating OCR text at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19026)
*David Lowry-Duda,Matteo Cargnelutti,Catherine Brobston,Salwa Ismail,Greg Leppert,Amanda Watson,Jonathan Zittrain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"增强文本"(Enriched Text, ET)方法处理哈佛图书馆机构书籍数据集(IB-HL)，通过HTML式标注在保留文本的同时维护元数据，支持按段落语言检测、重复段落识别和比特率评分，发布包含217B tokens、1.39B标注段落的IB-HL-ET版本，使用户可按需自定义处理而非接受统一编辑决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对IB-HL数据集，标准大规模预处理实践与仔细信息管理之间存在张力：现有流水线多为网页文本优化，往往激进过滤、去重、语言限制并丢弃有用元数据；研究人员在使用IB-HL时重复进行类似处理和分析工作，缺乏统一高效的处理方案。

Method: 提出"增强文本"方法，核心步骤包括：1)分离文首文尾内容；2)按段落检测语言；3)识别重复段落簇；4)计算每段落比特/字节评分；5)通过HTML式标注层将上述信息叠加在文本上，支持所有约250种语言的处理。用户通过解析这些标注可定制输出。

Result: 发布IB-HL-ET数据集(217B o200k_base tokens，983,003卷，1.39B标注子主题段落)及生成该数据集的流水线，涵盖原IB-HL全部983,004卷中的983,003卷。

Conclusion: 增强文本方法使数据集更易于机器解析和人类研究，用户可根据自身需求定制处理结果，而非接受全局编辑决策，解决了标准预处理与信息 stewardship 之间的冲突，并减少了研究人员的重复劳动。

Abstract: Released in 2025, Institutional Books: Harvard Library (IB-HL) is a collection of 983,004 volumes (242B o200k_base tokens), originally digitized through Harvard Library's participation in the Google Books Library project. As researchers and developers have begun to use IB-HL, a tension has emerged between standard large-scale preprocessing practices and the goals of careful information stewardship. Many existing pipelines optimize for web text: as a result, they tend to aggressively filter, deduplicate, restrict by language, and sometimes discard meaningful metadata. Meanwhile, researchers seeking to use IB-HL duplicate effort while performing similar processing and analysis.
  We describe an approach that we call Enriched Text. Instead of producing a single 'complete' stream of tokens, we normalize the text while preserving metadata through annotations. We separate endmatter, detect per-paragraph language, identify clusters of duplicate paragraphs, and compute per-paragraph bits-per-byte scores. We provide this information through HTML-like annotations layered on top of the text. By parsing these annotations, users can tailor the output to their own needs instead of accepting a global editorial decision on content. The pipeline applies to all $\approx$250 languages in the collection.
  This report describes this project's goals, implementation, and design rationale. The release includes IB-HL-ET (an enriched-text version of IB-HL containing 217B o200k_base tokens across 983,003 volumes, organized into 1.39B annotated subtopic paragraphs) and the pipeline that produced it. These serve to make the collection easier for machines to parse and for humans to study.

</details>


### [152] [Intercepting the Kangaroo: Experimental Astrolinguistics with Constructed Lexicons, Active Probing, and Large Language Models as Informants and Hypothesis Proposers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19124)
*Francesco Cordella,Mauro Cappelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Astrolinguistics -- communication with minds that categorize reality differently from ours -- has been purely speculative since Freudenthal's Lincos (1960). We make it experimental. Two language models with deliberately incompatible constructed lexicons (one encoding shape, color, and motion; the other fusing color with motion, encoding parity, and lacking shape) serve as informants with complete ground truth, while a fully scripted orchestrator translates between the two category systems. The central failure mode is the kangaroo effect: the silent attachment of a word to the wrong referent -- Quine's indeterminacy of translation, operationalized. Across 400+ simulated and live runs, a protocol combining cross-situational elimination, pre-registered predictive probes, active scene selection, a stricter recovery round, and quarantine produced no undetected mistranslations under the tested conditions and exceeded a passive baseline's coverage (d = 0.62). Injected kangaroo traps defeated naive ostension and pure statistical learning in 100% of runs, while the full protocol intercepted every decoy and, where discriminating evidence is ontologically unavailable, declared Quinean equivalence classes instead of guessing. Under informant noise it degrades gracefully: zero kangaroos persist up to 2% per-word noise; at 10% the protocol predominantly abstains rather than errs. Finally, words outside the scripted hypothesis space (a history-dependent relational term and an XOR contextual homonym) are recovered by a generate-and-test loop in which an LLM proposes rules and the script verifies them: coverage scales with proposer capability (0% -> 18% -> 72% -> 100%) while undetected mistranslations stayed at zero throughout. In the tested conditions, correctness is a property of the protocol; coverage is a property of the instruments.

</details>


### [153] [Comment-level Topic Drift Analysis in the Reddit Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19133)
*Steven Morse,Daniel Runfola,Trenton W. Ford*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于嵌入的动态主题建模方法，用于在大规模语料中检测和量化评论级别的主题漂移，通过分析127亿条Reddit评论发现政治和社会争议性话题存在显著的方向性漂移，而音乐和体育等领域相对稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要对大规模动态文本语料中的语义漂移和话语演变进行系统化分析，以理解主题如何随时间变化，尤其在社会争议性话题中，现有方法难以处理如此规模的动态嵌入空间分析。

Method: 利用预训练语言模型生成短文本的上下文语义嵌入，对127亿条Reddit评论（2006-2022）进行无监督分析，识别动态主题簇；修改现有方法以支持大规模分析；提出零模型比较检验以过滤虚假动态。

Result: 政治和社会争议性话题在嵌入空间中表现出显著的方向性漂移，主题间距离随时间系统性变化，超出零模型可解释范围；音乐和体育领域相对稳定。

Conclusion: 该方法有效揭示了大规模社交媒体文本中语义漂移的模式，为理解动态话语演变提供了新工具，并表明不同话题领域在稳定性上存在差异。

Abstract: We present a novel application of embedding-based dynamic topic modeling techniques to detect and quantify topic drift at the comment level in a massive corpus. By leveraging pretrained language models to generate contextualized semantic embeddings for short text, we analyzed 12.7 billion Reddit comments spanning 2006 to 2022. Using unsupervised methods on these embeddings, we identify dynamically evolving topic clusters over time. Our primary contribution is a methodology for analysis of semantic drift and discourse evolution in the embedding space itself. We also demonstrate modifications to existing methods that enable this analysis at scale, and we propose and demonstrate a null model comparison test to filter spurious dynamics. Key findings suggest that politically and socially contentious topics exhibit significant directional drift in embedding space, with inter-topic distances changing systematically over time beyond what the null model can explain, whereas domains such as music and sports remain comparatively stable.

</details>


### [154] [ChildSafeAds Shared Task 2026: Commercial Content in Child-Facing YouTube Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19165)
*Thales Bertaglia,Catalina Goanta,Gerasimos Spanakis,Gunes Acar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ChildSafeAds是一项关于YouTube视频中可能触达儿童和青少年的商业内容的共享任务。该研究构建了包含3,360个视频（来自939个频道）的数据集，基于SponsorBlock平台收集的赞助片段，结合视频转录、频道信息和链接页面等多源证据，设计三个子任务：识别推广类型(ST1)、分配产品类别(ST2)和识别法律风险标志(ST3)。研究发现45.5%的视频未正确使用平台内置的广告披露方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着YouTube平台向儿童和青少年群体推送的商业内容日益增多，需要系统性地识别和分析这些内容中的商业推广信息，特别是评估其合规性和潜在法律风险。现有缺乏专门针对儿童可访问的商业内容进行系统化标注和分析的任务。

Method: 研究者构建了ChildSafeAds数据集，包含3,360个YouTube视频片段。每个实例从SponsorBlock平台获取的赞助片段开始，配对可用的视频转录、视频和频道信息，以及视频描述中链接的销售或服务页面。证据分为四个累积访问级别，便于比较数据收集成本。使用GPT-5.4生成标签，经专家组织者团队审核样本并迭代优化分类法、提示词和模型选择；GPT-5.6-luna独立标注开发集。

Result: 构建的数据集中，45.5%的视频未能正确使用平台内置的"包含付费推广"标签方法。建立了三级任务框架：ST1确定推广的优惠类型，ST2分配产品类别，ST3识别法律风险标志。证据按四个累积访问级别组织，从视频转录到链接页面，支持不同数据收集成本下的结果比较。

Conclusion: ChildSafeAds为分析YouTube平台上面向儿童和青少年的商业内容提供了系统性框架。通过多源证据整合和层级化任务设计，能够全面评估商业推广的类型、分类和合规性。研究揭示了近半数视频存在广告披露不充分的问题，强调了监管和平台政策执行的重要性。未来更新版本将补充参与系统和共享任务结果。

Abstract: ChildSafeAds is a shared task on commercial content in YouTube videos likely to reach children and teenagers. It contains 3,360 videos from 939 channels. Each instance begins with a segment submitted to SponsorBlock, an open-source crowdsourced browser extension whose users mark sponsor segments so that others can skip them. We pair the segment with its available transcript, video and channel information, and a sales or service page linked from the video description. Systems determine what kind of offer is being promoted (ST1), assign product categories (ST2), and identify legal risk flags (ST3). The evidence is divided into four cumulative access levels, from the transcript to the linked page, so results can be compared against the cost of collecting the data. 45.5\% of videos in our data failed to properly use the in-platform ad disclosure method (the ``Includes paid promotion'' label). GPT-5.4 produced the labels after the expert organiser team reviewed samples and iterated on the taxonomy, prompts and model choices. GPT-5.6-luna independently labelled the development set. This report describes the task, data and evaluation. An updated version will add participating systems and shared-task results.

</details>


### [155] [SPADE: Self-Play in Adaptive Synthetic Executable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19197)
*Bo Liu,Simon Yu,Yiding Jiang,Ao Qu,Andrew Zhao,Zichen Liu,Junsu Kim,Zijian Zhou,Seungone Kim,Tongzheng Ren,Mickel Liu,Hanfei Yu,Zhaorun Chen,Weiyan Shi,Paul Pu Liang,Luke Zettlemoyer,Yejin Choi,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPADE (Self-Play in Adaptive Synthetic Executable Environments) 是一个自我对弈强化学习框架，单个LLM同时扮演环境设计者和推理智能体两个角色，通过动态生成可执行训练环境实现开放式自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续自我改进需要不断扩展的、多样化的自适应目标池。现有训练环境（手工策划、静态合成或冻结验证器）在模型扩展时保持目标分布固定，限制了智能体的持续学习能力。

Method: SPADE框架中单个LLM扮演双重角色：环境设计者（用可执行代码编写完整长周期训练环境，具有OpenAI Gym风格的reset()/step()接口）和推理智能体（在这些环境中学习行动）。使用基于奖励差距的后悔估计来优化环境设计，使环境设计者学习到将环境定位在智能体能力边界上但保持可行。关键成功组件包括：基于大规模预训练语料库样本接地环境设计者，并赋予其累积环境记忆。

Result: 在30B参数模型上，SPADE在8个数学、科学、代码和推理基准测试中平均提升+5.3，在工具使用设置中BFCL-v4多轮任务提升+5.7，ACEBench-Agent提升+13.9。在游戏设置中，与最强基线的差距随模型规模增长而扩大。环境设计本身成为可学习组件。

Conclusion: 通过将环境设计本身作为可学习组件，SPADE向开放式自我改进迈出了具体的一步，为语言智能体提供了动态生成训练环境的能力，突破静态环境分布的限制。

Abstract: Continuous self-improvement requires an ever-expanding pool of self-generated, diverse, adaptive goals. For language agents, existing training environment pools (hand-curated, statically synthesized, or frozen-verifier) keep the goal distribution fixed as the learner scales. We introduce SPADE (Self-Play in Adaptive Synthetic Executable Environments), a self-play RL framework in which a single LLM plays two roles: an Environment Designer that writes complete, long-horizon training environments as executable code with an OpenAI Gym-style reset()/step() interface, and a Reasoning Agent that learns to act in them. Each is a stateful, multi-turn environment (state transitions, reward functions, and verification code), so one interface spans reasoning problems and multi-step agentic tool use. The Reasoning Agent's regret is estimated using the gap between its reward with and without privileged hints; in optimizing this regret signal the Environment Designer learns to target environments at the edge of the agent's capabilities while keeping them feasible. Through extensive experimentation, we find several components critical to success: grounding the Environment Designer on documents sampled from a large pretraining corpus, and giving it an accumulated environment memory. Scaling to 30B-parameter models, SPADE improves over the strongest fixed-environment baseline by +5.3 on average across eight held-out math, science, code, and reasoning benchmarks, and lifts the tool-use setting by +5.7 on BFCL-v4 multi-turn and +13.9 on ACEBench-Agent; on the games setting, the margin over the strongest baseline grows with model scale. By making environment design itself a learnable component, SPADE takes a concrete step toward open-ended self-improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Scheduling and Routing with Degradation-Triggered Job Arrivals: An Application to Forest Firefighting with an Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Fleet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18140)
*Erdi Dasdemir,Esther Jose,Rajan Batta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文定义了一个交织的调度与路由问题，即现有作业退化会引发新作业。以航空森林灭火为背景，旨在通过及时处理来最大化剩余价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于"防微杜渐"（a stitch in time saves nine）的理念，具有实际应用价值。具体应用于航空森林灭火场景，其中延误干预会导致火势扩大、区域价值降低并波及邻近区域。

Method: 开发了一个混合整数规划模型以最大化受威胁区域的保留价值，并采用基于动态约束生成的混合模型来提高模型的可扩展性。

Result: 通过计算实验和案例研究评估了所提出模型的性能和实用性。

Conclusion: 为了保障研究的可重复性并促进后续研究，公开提供了模型的代码库。

Abstract: We define an intertwined scheduling and routing problem where new jobs appear due to the degradation of the existing jobs. Specifically, once a job arrives at a potential job location, a time window begins during which the demand of the job can be fulfilled. The demand degrades within the time window, and once it surpasses a particular threshold, it triggers the arrival of new jobs. Each job location inherently possesses an initial default reward, and the presence of an unprocessed job at a location gradually reduces this default value. The overall objective is to maximize the total remaining reward. The underlying motivation of this problem aligns with the proverb ``a stitch in time saves nine," and the problem itself carries practical implications. We focus on the problem in the context of aerial forest firefighting. Each ignited area has a designated action window; delaying intervention causes the fire to grow, diminishing the area's value and causing it to spread to adjacent areas. We develop a mixed-integer programming model that maximizes value retention in wildfire-threatened regions, and a hybrid model based on dynamic constraint generation to enhance the scalability of the model. We evaluate the performance and practicality of our models through computational experiments and a case study. Additionally, we ensure the study's reproducibility and encourage further research by providing open access to the codebase of our model.

</details>


### [157] [Trust as a Field: A Macroscopic Representation for Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18178)
*Md Mahmudul Islam,Shaurya Agarwal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时空信任场框架，将微观车辆级信任聚合为连续的空间时间表示，并研究如何从稀疏的路侧单元（RSU）测量数据中重构完整的信任场，发现基于场信息的深度学习方法比基于坐标的基线方法更准确地恢复与轨迹对齐的低信任模式，重建误差更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信任评估是合作与联网车辆系统的基本组成部分，但现有方法主要在单车层面运作，难以推理跨路段的信任演化。

Method: 提出时空信任场框架，将微观车辆级信任聚合为连续的空间时间表示；在路段上正式定义信任场；使用合成轨迹进行仿真实验，在简单道路场景下分析信任场行为；研究从稀疏RSU测量中重构完整信任场的应用，比较了两种深度学习方法：(1)学习通用信任场的基于坐标的深度学习基线；(2)将信任视为由车辆携带的潜在量并通过聚合机制强制测量一致性的基于场信息的深度学习方法。

Result: 基于场信息的深度学习方法比基于坐标的基线方法更准确地恢复与轨迹对齐的低信任模式，并产生改进的重建误差。

Conclusion: 将信任视为由车辆携带的潜在量并通过聚合机制强制测量一致性的基于场信息的方法，在从稀疏测量数据重构信任场方面优于通用的基于坐标的学习方法。

Abstract: Trust assessment is a fundamental component of cooperative and connected vehicle systems. However, existing approaches operate primarily at the level of individual vehicles, making it difficult to reason about trust evolution across road segments. In this paper, we propose a spatio-temporal trust-field framework that aggregates microscopic vehicle-level trust into a continuous representation over space and time. The trust field is formally defined on road segments. We conducted simulation-based experiments using synthetic trajectories generated under controlled conditions, enabling analysis of trust-field behavior in simple road scenarios. Beyond theoretical modeling, we study an implication of the trust-field concept: reconstructing the full trust field from sparse roadside-unit (RSU) measurements. We compare (i) a coordinate-based deep learning baseline that learns a generic trust field from sparse samples and (ii) a field-informed deep learning method that treats trust as a latent quantity carried by vehicles and enforces measurement consistency through the aggregation mechanism. The field-informed approach more accurately recovers trajectory-aligned low-trust patterns and yields improved reconstruction error.

</details>


### [158] [Revisiting the "Push-T" Robot Manipulation Task with Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18227)
*Shuangyu Xie,Kaiyuan Chen,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文重新审视Push-T基准任务，使用LLM编码智能体（Claude Code）创建无需演示数据的算法解决方案，实现100%成功率且比使用200个人类演示训练的扩散策略步骤减少46%，并扩展到Push-A到Push-Z任务及3D跨形态仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究新兴智能机器人技术中LLM编码智能体在解决经典操作学习基准任务Push-T上的有效性，探索无需演示数据的算法方案，并与传统基于视觉运动模仿学习的策略进行对比。

Method: 采用Claude Code配合Fable 5作为LLM编码智能体，通过提示词引导其创建Push-T任务的算法解决方案。智能体自行发现2D gym仿真环境，利用仿真实验学习推物力学，并通过迭代优化改进策略。

Result: 智能体达到100%成功率，使用的步骤数比使用200个人类演示训练的最佳扩散策略少46%。成功将T扩展至完整字母表（Push-A到Push-Z），使用自生成课程学习，并为Franka和UR5机械臂生成了带视觉反馈的3D跨形态仿真代码。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的编码智能体方法能够在无需演示数据的情况下有效解决Push-T任务及其扩展变体，在样本效率上优于传统模仿学习方法，展示了智能机器人在操作学习中的潜力。

Abstract: Push-T is an iconic benchmark for learning manipulation policies from human demonstrations. The robot must use a single point of contact to push a T-shaped block into a target pose. In this short paper, we revisit the Push-T task in the context of emerging advances in Agentic Robotics where an LLM coding agent -- Claude Code with Fable 5 -- is prompted to create an algorithmic solution that does not require any demonstration data. We study how effective the agentic coding loop can solve the Push-T task, and compare the resulting code as policy with the visuomotor imitation learning policy. Results suggest that the agent found the 2D gym simulation online, and used sim experiments to learn push mechanics, iteratively optimizing to achieve 100% success rate using 46% fewer steps than the best diffusion policy trained with 200 human demonstrations. The coding agent also solve extensions from T to the full alphabet (Push-A to Push-Z) using a self generated curriculum and generated simulation code for the Franka and UR5 robot arms in 3D cross-embodiment simulations with visual feedback. Videos, policies and details will be posted online.

</details>


### [159] [GigaBrain-WBC-0.5: A Behavior World Model for Robust Whole-Body Control with Environment Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18234)
*Ziyang Cheng,Tianshu Tang,Jinxin Lan,Xinze Chen,Yuhan Gong,Zhichao Liu,Changzhong Wu,Yahao Mao,Zongyan Deng,Mingxuan Ma,Huasen Xi,Yilong Liu,Yutong Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Yang Wang,Yun Ye,Guan Huang,Xiaojie Jin,Zheng Zhu,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GigaBrain-WBC-0.5 introduces a Behavior World Model (BWM) for humanoid whole-body control, using a causal Transformer to predict actions, states, and latent behavior commands, achieving robust terrain interaction, command rejection, and fall recovery.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing whole-body trackers work only on flat ground, failing to model contact dynamics and relying on expanding reference-motion corpora, which breaks when feasible behaviors become environment-dependent.

Method: A causal Transformer is trained to jointly predict next action, next state, and distribution over next latent behavior command. An automatic terrain-annotation pipeline extracts 3D contact geometry from retargeted motion. At deployment, the predicted distribution detects and retracts implausible commands onto learned behaviors.

Result: GigaBrain-WBC-0.5 achieves highest success rates: 81.3% terrain interaction (4.3x baseline), 83.1% under implausible commands, 99.3% fall recovery (16.8x baseline). Hardware trials show robustness under disturbances and missing supports, with cross-robot transfer via fine-tuning.

Conclusion: The Behavior World Model provides a unified, robust humanoid control policy that integrates real-time commands, environment interaction, and automatic recovery from implausible commands, falls, and disturbances, significantly outperforming existing trackers.

Abstract: Whole-body motion tracking policies turn a humanoid into a robust control interface: the teleoperator---or an upstream model---only supplies a coarse movement intent, while the low-level policy keeps the robot balanced and physically feasible. Existing trackers deliver this interface only on flat ground: trained in empty scenes, they never learn how contact with terrain and objects reshapes their dynamics, and they attempt to teach the policy to balance under any command by continually enlarging the reference-motion corpus, which stops working once feasible behaviors become environment-dependent. We present GigaBrain-WBC-0.5, the first Behavior World Model (BWM) for humanoid whole-body control. Rather than a purely reactive tracker, we train a causal Transformer to jointly predict its next action, next state, and the distribution over its next latent behavior command, so the network that acts also models how the environment shapes what it can do next. An automatic terrain-annotation pipeline recovers full 3D contact geometry from retargeted motion, enabling terrain annotation at the scale of existing motion datasets. The predicted distribution is reused at deployment to detect implausible commands online and retract them onto learned behaviors, so the robot attempts tasks in a "best-effort" manner. The result is a unified policy that takes real-time command, interacts with environment, and stays robust to implausible commands, falls, and disturbances. GigaBrain-WBC-0.5 achieves the highest success rate across all four regimes among three large-scale tracker baselines: 81.3% on terrain interaction (4.3x the strongest baseline), 83.1% under implausible commands, and 99.3% fall recovery (16.8x the strongest baseline). Hardware trials show robust interaction under missing supports and disturbances; the Unitree G1 checkpoint transfers to the Maker L01 robot with simple fine-tuning.

</details>


### [160] [GAPL: Grounded Action-effect Policy Learning for LLM-Based Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18254)
*Zhihong Cui,Hengyu Liu,Zhangkai Wu,Yushuai Li,Tianyi Li,Peiyuan Guan,Amir Taherkordi,Tor Skeie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAPL框架将LLM的效果估计与仿真环境动态结合，通过闭环系统实现自动驾驶轨迹规划，显著提升性能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽具备语义丰富的规划能力，但在自动驾驶中面临幻觉推理、环境动态接地能力差以及控制数值精度有限等挑战，需要结合环境真实性和控制精度的统一框架。

Method: GAPL由三个模块构成：(1)基于LLM的效果评估器进行结构化多维度动作-效果估计；(2)基于仿真的效果接地器通过仿真器运行预测符合动力学的动态效果；(3)效果感知决策器通过蒸馏器将LLM效果估计与仿真结果对齐，指导基于PPO的策略学习。

Result: 在四个Highway-env场景的实验中，GAPL一致优于基线方法，碰撞率平均降低0.76，平均位移误差(ADE)降低0.86，最终位移误差(FDE)降低2.00，奖励平均提升1.44。

Conclusion: GAPL通过整合LLM语义理解与仿真环境动态接地，有效克服了纯LLM方法的局限性，为自动驾驶轨迹规划提供了性能更优的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Trajectory planning for autonomous driving requires both high-level reasoning and precise low-level control. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer semantic-rich planning capabilities, however, their application is limited by hallucinated reasoning, poor grounding in environment dynamics, and limited numerical precision in control. We propose GAPL (Grounded Action-effect Policy Learning), a unified framework that integrates LLM-based effect estimation, simulation-based effect grounding, and policy optimization into a closed-loop system. GAPL consists of three modules: (1) an LLM-based Effect Evaluator for structured multi-dimensional action-effect estimation; (2) a Simulation-based Effect Grounder that predicts dynamics-consistent effects from simulator rollouts; and (3) an Effect-Aware Decision Maker that grounds LLM effect estimates against simulation via a distiller to guide Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)-based policy learning. Experiments on four Highway-env scenarios demonstrate that GAPL consistently outperforms baselines, achieving average reductions of {0.76, 0.86, 2.00} in collision rate, average displacement error (ADE), and final displacement error (FDE), and an average reward gain of 1.44.

</details>


### [161] [VERAGMIL: Virtual Environment for Scooping Granular Foods with Imitation Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18258)
*Amanuel Ergogo,Diego Dall'Alba,Przemyslaw Korzeniowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VERAGMIL框架，通过高保真模拟器与VR接口相结合，为机器人辅助喂食（RAF）系统处理颗粒状食物提供有效的模仿学习训练方法。实验表明VR采集的演示数据优于3D鼠标数据，其中BCQ算法表现最佳，显著降低洒落率并接近人类专家水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助喂食（RAF）系统对帮助残疾人或运动障碍者完成进食任务至关重要。然而，操作米饭、豆类等颗粒状食物因物理特性动态变化而极具挑战性。虽然从人类演示中学习是可行的解决方案，但获取高质量演示数据非常复杂，需要新的方法来改善演示数据的采集质量。

Method: VERAGMIL框架结合高保真模拟器与直观VR界面，用于记录演示并支持多种模仿学习方法。该框架提供逼真的训练环境，包含机器人、传感器和具有不同物理特性的食物物品。作者使用VR接口和3D空间鼠标两种演示方式训练BC、BC-RNN和BCQ三种模仿学习模型，并在颗粒状舀取和运输任务中评估其性能。

Result: VR基界面采集的演示数据显著优于3D空间鼠标数据。在成功率、洒落率、对未见食物类型的泛化能力以及任务完成时间等指标上，BCQ算法表现最佳，尤其在减少洒落方面接近人类专家水平。三种模型在颗粒状食物处理任务中均展现出不同程度的可行性。

Conclusion: VERAGMIL框架有效验证了VR界面在采集高质量模仿学习演示数据方面的优势，为RAF系统处理颗粒状食物提供了可靠训练方案。实验结果证明该方法能够显著提升机器人操作颗粒状食物的性能，接近人类专家水平，为未来RAF系统发展奠定了重要基础。框架代码已公开共享以促进进一步研究。

Abstract: Robot-Assisted Feeding (RAF) systems are essential for assisting individuals with disabilities or motor impairments in eating tasks. Manipulating granular food items, such as rice and beans, poses significant challenges due to their dynamic physical properties. Learning from human demonstrations offers a promising solution, but acquiring high-quality demonstrations is complex. To address this, we present VERAGMIL, a framework that combines a high-fidelity simulator with an intuitive Virtual Reality (VR) interface for recording demonstrations and supporting different imitation learning methods. VERAGMIL provides a realistic environment for training RAF systems to handle granular materials, including robots, sensors, and various food items with distinct physical characteristics. We evaluate VERAGMIL by training three imitation learning models, BC, BC-RNN, and BCQ, on granular scooping and transporting tasks using both VR interface and 3D space mouse demonstrations, comparing them with a human-expert baseline. The models are assessed on success rate, spillage, generalization to unseen food items, and task completion time. Results show that VR-based demonstrations significantly outperform 3D space mouse data, with BCQ achieving the best overall performance, particularly in reducing spillage and approaching human performance. These findings underscore the effectiveness of our framework for training RAF systems in granular material handling. The code for our framework is publicly available at: https://github.com/AmanuelErgogo/VERAGMIL.git.

</details>


### [162] [Transferable Tool-Tissue Contact Detection from Stereo Depth in Robot-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18270)
*Mingyeung Wu,Zhonghao Zhang,Hao Yang,Alan Kuntz,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度图的最小距离标量信号，结合隐马尔可夫模型，实现机器人手术中工具-组织接触的高泛化检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠工具-组织接触检测对机器人手术中的交互感知控制和力估计至关重要；现有基于RGB外观的分类器泛化能力弱，需要一种更鲁棒、可迁移的接触线索。

Method: 从立体对生成深度图，在工具边界周围提取空间支持的最小距离补丁，并将其压缩为标量 -log10|d|；将该信号建模为完全监督的二状态隐马尔可夫模型，采用六折留一会话外推（LOSO）集成训练，阈值基于折外预测确定。

Result: 在四个保留会话（相同任务同幻影、相同任务不同幻影、不同任务不同幻影）上测试，模型取得宏观F1=0.927、AUPRC=0.980；与重现的RGB基线相比，RGB模型仅在同类任务上表现好（F1=0.965），跨任务/跨幻影时性能大幅下降（宏观F1=0.320）。

Conclusion: 工具-组织距离是一个强且可迁移的接触检测线索，相比RGB外观能显著提升跨任务、跨对象的泛化能力，为机器人手术中的接触感知提供了更鲁棒的方案。

Abstract: Reliable tool--tissue contact detection can support interaction-aware control and downstream force estimation in robot-assisted surgery. Most existing methods learn a contact classifier from RGB appearance, which is hard to generalize. In this work, we use the depth image generated from a stereo pair to give more information about tool--tissue contact. For each depth frame, we localize a spatially supported minimum-distance patch around the tool boundary and reduce it to a single scalar, $-\log_{10}|d|$; this signal rises and falls in step with ground-truth contact. We formalize this observation with a fully supervised two-state hidden Markov model. We fit this model as a six-fold leave-one-session-out (LOSO) ensemble on six palpation sessions against a single silicone cup-like phantom, with the decision threshold selected from the pooled out-of-fold predictions. It is evaluated on four held-out sessions of three categories: 1. same task on same phantom; 2. same task on different phantom; 3. different task on different phantom. This model reaches held-out macro F1 $0.927$ and AUPRC $0.980$. We further compare against a reproduction of an RGB-based contact classifier from prior work. This RGB-based model achieves high performance on the first category (F1 $0.965$), but substantially lower performance on the other two, resulting in macro F1 $0.320$ across all four sessions. These results indicate that the tool--tissue distance is a strong, transferable cue for contact detection in robot-assisted surgery.

</details>


### [163] [GuideFetch: A Task Coordination Framework for Concurrent Navigation and Object Retrieval in Assistive Robot Dogs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18292)
*Qian Yin,Ruiping Liu,Kunyu Peng,Jianxiang Man,Isik Baran Sandan,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Di Wen,Kailun Yang,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GuideFetch协调框架，用于两个异构机器狗同时执行导航引导和物体取送任务。实验结果表明，与顺序执行相比，并行执行在相似成功率下可将任务完成时间缩短41.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在盲用户使用机器导盲犬寻找座位、另一只辅助机器狗同时取咖啡的场景中，导航与物体取送任务可以并行重叠执行。仅语法有效的LLM计划可能违反具身约束，看似成功的控制器运动本身并不能证明任务完成。

Method: 提出GuideFetch协调框架：1）LLM从自然语言指令实例化调度条件的四动作模式；2）执行前规范化机器人、技能和目标别名，验证提议动作与已注册目标、机器人能力及所选调度的一致性；3）机器人和物体状态控制顺序与并行执行；4）在90种场景-种子组合（360次执行）的2×2匹配研究中评估。

Result: 1）所有180个在线LLM响应均无需回退或重放即可验证，且与相应脚本化计划匹配；2）顺序执行和并行执行分别达到72/90和71/90的操作性成功；3）在56个两种调度均完成的情况中，并行执行将平均完工时间减少41.3%。

Conclusion: 在该受控环境下，角色专门化和动作重叠可缩短任务完成时间，而状态检查能够区分计划有效性与已验证的任务完成。代码将开源。

Abstract: Consider a robot guide dog escorting a blind user to an available seat while a second assistive robot dog concurrently retrieves a cup of coffee and delivers it to the same seat. This setting motivates concurrent execution because navigation and object retrieval can overlap. A syntactically valid Large Language Model (LLM) plan may still violate embodiment constraints, and successful-looking controller motion does not by itself establish task completion. We introduce \textsc{GuideFetch}, a coordination framework for concurrent navigation and object retrieval by a heterogeneous guider and fetcher team. An LLM instantiates a schedule-conditioned four-action schema from a natural-language instruction. Before execution, robot, skill, and target aliases are normalized, and proposed actions are validated against registered targets, robot capabilities, and the selected schedule. Robot and object states then govern sequential and parallel execution. In a matched $2\times2$ study across 90 combinations of scene and seed (360 executions), all 180 online LLM responses validate without fallback or replay and match the corresponding scripted plans. For each planner source, sequential and parallel execution achieve $72/90$ and $71/90$ operational successes, respectively. Among the 56 cases completed by both schedules, parallel execution reduces mean makespan by 41.3\%. Within this controlled setting, role specialization and action overlap shorten completed missions, while state checks distinguish plan validity from verified mission completion. Source code will be available.

</details>


### [164] [A Task-Agnostic Control Strategy for Dynamic Assistance with Pneumatically Actuated Soft Exosuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18364)
*Anoush Sepehri,Zachary Huang,Raymond de Callafon,Michael T. Tolley,Tania K. Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种用于气动软外骨骼的逆植物控制策略，该策略仅依靠运动学传感即可在日常活动中提供任务无关的动态辅助。控制器可将交互扭矩减少高达73%，并将主要屈肌和伸肌的激活度降低高达47%，无需生理或力传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气动人工肌肉为康复、增强和辅助日常生活的上肢软外骨骼开发提供了新机遇，但其复杂的动力学特性和有限的带宽使得基于用户意图的响应性辅助成为长期挑战。

Method: 研究采用Hammerstein动态模型对机器人系统进行建模，该模型包含Preisach磁滞模型和线性时不变滤波器，以捕捉系统的静态和动态行为。通过140秒的数据对每个用户进行个性化建模，并近似反函数以集成到控制回路中。系统仅依赖运动学传感。

Result: 在模拟手腕软辅助外骨骼的测试台上评估表明，控制器在无辅助条件下将交互扭矩降低高达73%，将主要屈肌和伸肌激活度降低高达47%，适用速度范围为8°/s至120°/s。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一种控制策略，可为气动软外骨骼提供任务无关、动态辅助，无需生理或力传感器即可解读用户意图。

Abstract: Pneumatic artificial muscles have provided new opportunities to develop upper-extremity soft exosuits for reha- bilitation, augmentation, and assisted daily living. However, the complex dynamics and limited bandwidth of these actuators has made providing responsive assistance based on user intention a longstanding challenge. In this work, we present an inverse-plant control strategy for pneumatically actuated soft exosuits that only relies on kinematic sensing for task-agnostic and dynamic assistance during daily living. We model the human-robot system using a Hammerstein dynamic model, consisting of a Preisach hysteresis model and a linear time-invariant filter, to capture the static and dynamic behavior of the system. We personalize our model to each user using 140 s of data and approximate an inverse to integrate into our control loop. When evaluated on a test rig that emulated a soft assistive exosuit for the wrist, our controller reduced the interaction torque by up to 73% and the activation of key flexor and extensor muscles by up to 47% relative to the condition with no assistance for speeds ranging from 8°/s to 120°/s. Overall, this work presents a control strategy that can provide task-agnostic, dynamic assistance with pneumatically actuated soft exosuits without the need for physiological or force sensors to interpret user intention.

</details>


### [165] [The Embodiment Gap in Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18433)
*Yukiyasu Domae,Keisuke Shirai,Hanbit Oh,Ryoichi Nakajo,Tomohiro Motoda,Koshi Makihara,Masaki Murooka,Takuma Yagi,Yoshiaki Bando,Ryo Hanai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本综述研究了机器人基础模型在不同具身之间的可迁移性问题，提出了"具身鸿沟"（embodiment gap）概念——即通用模型/表征/数据与在特定目标机器人上实际执行之间的差距，并提供了分类框架和分析维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型（包括视觉-语言-动作策略）常被从扩展视角讨论：更多数据、更大模型、更广基准应能改善泛化能力。然而，在机器人领域，即使模型能够泛化，在其能够在具有特定躯体的机器人上运行之前，仍有许多工作要做。这些工作因方法和目标机器人而异，且差异影响实际部署。因此，需要一个框架来理解和分类这种"具身鸿沟"。

Method: （1）在二维映射上放置现有方法：一个轴显示共享结构的类型，另一个轴显示在目标机器人上执行所需适应的阶段；（2）通过三个重叠的研究方向审查近期工作：共享语义和感知、共享机器人数据和接口、学习跨具身的对应关系；（3）提出一个适应工作的报告框架，该框架能揭示仅凭成功率无法体现的信息。

Result: 综述识别了在不同机器人具身之间可以复用的内容以及在新机器人上必须实现的内容；对现有方法进行了分类；指出了比较跨具身学习时需要检查的工作，并强调了在新机器人上仍需完成的工作以及未来研究的问题。

Conclusion: 理解"具身鸿沟"对于机器人基础模型的实际部署至关重要。即使模型具备泛化能力，在特定机器人上仍需大量工作。需要更系统的报告框架来评估跨具身学习工作的完整性，不能仅依赖成功率作为衡量标准。

Abstract: Robot foundation models (RFMs), including vision-language-action (VLA) policies, are often discussed through a scaling view: more data, larger models, and broader benchmarks should improve generalization. In robotics, however, a model can generalize while work still remains before it can run on a robot with a particular body. The work required differs across methods and target robots, and those differences affect practical deployment. We call the gap between reusable models, representations, or data and their use in execution on the target robot the embodiment gap. This survey examines what can be reused across robot embodiments and what must still be implemented on a new robot. We place existing methods on a two-axis map that shows the type of shared structure and the stage at which adaptation is needed for execution on the target robot. We then examine recent work through three overlapping research directions: sharing semantics and perception, sharing robot data and interfaces, and learning correspondence across embodiments. We also propose a reporting framework for adaptation work that success rate alone does not reveal. The framework identifies the work that should be checked when comparing cross-embodiment learning and highlights work that remains on a new robot and questions for future study.

</details>


### [166] [HarvestPoint-ACT: Explicit Target Selection and Harvest-Point Conditioning for Robotic Fruit Harvesting under Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18446)
*Hanying Hu,Weipeng Li,Yikun Huang,Hao Chen,Zhengtao Hu,Changcai Yang,Weiwei Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HarvestPoint-ACT系统，通过实例分割和关键点检测结合的方法，解决机器人采摘中水果选择和夹爪关闭位置确定的问题。系统在可见水果上预测掩码和采摘点，调度器根据遮挡和移动距离进行排序，并在每次尝试后重新检测排序。在树冠模拟环境中，系统达到88%的成功率，在重度遮挡下仍保持75%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端模仿学习避免了手工设计机器人运动，但策略仍需决定采摘哪个水果以及在何处关闭夹爪。遮挡会导致策略在采摘过程中丢失选定的水果，且仅从像素难以推断正确的关闭位置。

Method: HarvestPoint-ACT系统将决策显式化：(1)采用带有关键点分支的实例分割前端，为每个可见水果预测掩码和采摘点；(2)调度器根据遮挡程度和移动距离对候选目标排序并选择目标；(3)每次尝试后重新检测并排序候选目标；(4)将选定水果编码为八维状态输入动作分块变换器，包含绝对采摘点、夹爪到该点的向量、有效性标志和置信度分数；(5)当选定水果暂时未检测到时，保留上次采摘点估计并标记为过时，若丢失持续则中止尝试。

Result: 在树冠模拟环境中，HarvestPoint-ACT系统实现88%的成功率，在重度遮挡条件下成功率为75%。

Conclusion: 该系统通过显式感知决策机制，有效解决了机器人采摘中的水果选择和夹爪定位问题，即使在遮挡条件下也能保持较高的采摘成功率，为农业机器人自动化提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: End-to-end imitation learning avoids hand-made robot motion for approaching and grasping, but the policy must still decide which fruit to pick and where to close the gripper. Occlusion can make the policy lose the selected fruit during harvesting, and the correct closing point is difficult to infer from pixels alone. This paper presents HarvestPoint-ACT, which makes both decisions explicit in perception and provides them to the policy. An instance segmentation front end with a keypoint branch predicts a mask and a harvest point for each visible fruit, where the harvest point specifies the location to close the gripper. A scheduler ranks detected candidates by occlusion and travel distance and selects one target. After each attempt, it redetects and reranks the candidates because the canopy may have changed. The selected fruit is encoded for an action chunking transformer as an eight-dimensional state, containing the absolute harvest point, the vector from the gripper to that point, a validity flag, and a confidence score. When the selected fruit is temporarily undetected, the system retains the last harvest point estimate in the robot base frame and marks it as stale, and aborts the attempt if the loss persists. On a canopy mock-up, HarvestPoint-ACT achieves a success rate of 88%, and of 75% under heavy occlusion.

</details>


### [167] [Backward Layout Search for Sequence-Constrained Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18454)
*Xi Zhang,Jiancong Dai,Hao Chen,Zhengtao Hu,Changcai Yang,Weiwei Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种逆向布局搜索方法，通过逆序分配零件初始位姿，利用装配步骤间的依赖关系，有效减少了搜索空间和计算时间，最终生成无碰撞的可执行装配布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装配布局规划需要确定装配位置和各零件的初始位姿，同时保证预设装配序列无碰撞执行。该问题的挑战在于：每个装配步骤后障碍物环境会变化，工作空间中未装配的零件可能阻碍机器人运动。

Method: 逆向布局搜索（BLS）：基于装配步骤可行性仅依赖当前及后续装配零件初始位姿这一特性，采用逆装配顺序分配零件初始位姿。每个扩展步骤执行几何、运动学、抓取和预设运动检查，利用碰撞掩码和候选集过滤剔除不可行的零件初始位姿候选。通过束选择保留有前景的部分布局，最后用正向装配顺序的完整运动规划验证完整布局。

Result: 在五个装配模型上的实验表明，BLS能够生成无碰撞的可执行布局，并且与匹配的正向搜索相比，减少了步骤评估次数和搜索时间。

Conclusion: BLS通过逆向搜索策略，充分利用装配步骤间的依赖关系，实现了高效的装配布局规划，在保证可执行性的同时显著降低了计算开销，为机器人装配布局问题提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Robotic assembly layout planning must determine the assembly site and the initial pose of each part while ensuring collision-free execution of a prescribed assembly sequence. This problem is challenging because the obstacle environment changes after each assembly step, and unassembled parts re maining in the workspace may block robot motions. We observe that the feasibility of each assembly step depends only on the initial poses of the current and later-assembled parts. Based on this dependency, we propose Backward Layout Search (BLS), which assigns initial part poses in reverse assembly order. Each expansion performs geometric, kinematic, grasp, and prescribed-motion checks, while collision masks and candidate set filtering remove infeasible initial part pose candidates. Promising partial layouts are retained through beam selection, and complete layouts are validated by full motion planning in forward assembly order. Experiments on five assembly models show that BLS produces collision-free executable layouts and reduces step evaluations and search time compared with a matched forward search.

</details>


### [168] [DevGRU: Depth-guided Visual Navigation using a Collision-aware Recurrent Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18470)
*Kyung Min Han,Eunsom Kim,Young J. Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DevGRU 是一种基于深度图像和点目标条件的导航系统，通过碰撞感知轨迹预测实现避障，在多个场景中显著优于现有方法，同时模型更小、推理更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉导航模型虽然追求通用性，但在复杂室内环境中（尤其结构化布局和狭窄通道）容易发生碰撞。因此，需要一种能有效避障且性能稳定的导航方法。

Method: 提出 DevGRU 系统，包含动作预测器（AP）和碰撞预测器。AP 生成碰撞感知的未来轨迹以避免即时障碍；碰撞预测器则补偿目标位姿估计误差，主动减少未来偏差。

Result: 在九个场景下，与 ViNT、NoMaD、NavDP 等基线对比，DevGRU 在导航性能上显著领先。同时，模型可训练参数较少，推理速度最快，相比 NavDP 模型大小减少 7 倍，推理时间缩短 17 倍。

Conclusion: DevGRU 通过碰撞感知轨迹预测和误差补偿机制，有效提升了复杂环境中的导航安全性和效率，在性能与计算开销之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Existing visual navigation models often aim to develop foundation models that can generalize robot navigation across diverse platforms. However, many of these models are prone to collisions when deployed in complex indoor environments, particularly in structured layouts and narrow passages. To address this problem, we propose a depth image- and point-goal-conditioned navigation system, DevGRU. The proposed system employs an action predictor (AP) that generates collision-aware future trajectories, enabling effective avoidance of immediate obstacles. In conjunction with a collision predictor, the AP further compensates for errors accumulated in the goal pose estimation and proactively mitigates future deviations. To evaluate our method, we conducted experiments across nine different scenes and three state-of-the-art approaches - ViNT, NoMaD, and NavDP - as well as four additional variants of ViNT and NoMaD. In terms of navigation performance, DevGRU significantly outperforms ViNT and NoMaD by a large margin. In addition, the proposed model has a relatively small number of trainable parameters, resulting in the fastest inference time among the baselines, particularly outperforming NavDP by 7x in model size and 17x in inference time.

</details>


### [169] [An Experimental Study of Downwash Effects on a Continuum Manipulator Integrated with a Multirotor UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18507)
*Anuraj Uthayasooriyan,Krishna Manaswi Digumarti,ernando Vanegas,Felipe Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了多旋翼平台上腱驱连续体机械臂的旋翼下洗气流引起的运动学偏差，发现垂直位置和偏航角偏差最大且受地面效应放大，并提出基于常数曲率引导的高斯过程回归残差模型来改进位姿预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机械臂空中操作系统在受限或危险环境中利用软机械臂的顺应性和灵巧性执行任务，但旋翼下洗气流会降低系统性能，特别是在墙壁和地面附近。这种下洗效应对于连续体机械臂尚未得到充分表征。

Method: 1. 在静止空气条件下，将连续体机械臂(CM)与常数曲率(CC)模型进行比较
2. 量化不同旋翼油门水平下自由空间中的末端执行器位姿偏差
3. 在墙壁附近和地面附近最大油门水平下测试偏差
4. 从实验数据中学习基于CC引导的GPR残差模型

Result: 1. 垂直位置和偏航角显示最大偏差
2. 地面效应放大这些偏差
3. 提出的CC引导GPR残差模型显著改善前向位姿预测RMSE：位置误差减少89-95%，方向误差减少47-79%

Conclusion: 本研究支持补偿导向、下洗感知的连续体机械臂空中操作系统建模方法，为这类系统的实际应用提供了更精确的运动学模型。

Abstract: Continuum arm aerial manipulation systems leverage soft-manipulator compliance and dexterity for tasks in confined or hazardous environments, but propeller downwash can degrade performance, particularly near walls and the ground. This effect remains uncharacterized for continuum manipulators. This letter experimentally studies downwash-induced kinematic deviations of a tendon-driven continuum manipulator integrated with a multirotor platform. Under still-air conditions, the CM is compared with a constant-curvature (CC) model. Downwash- induced end-effector pose deviations are then quantified relative to the mean still-air experimental baseline at four propeller throttle levels in free space, and at maximum throttle near a wall, and near the ground. Vertical position and yaw show the largest deviations and are amplified by ground effect. A CC-guided Gaussian process regression (GPR) residual model is learned from experimental data that improves forward pose prediction RMSE (position by 89-95%, orientation by 47-79%), and support compensation-oriented, downwash-aware modeling of continuum arm aerial manipulation systems.

</details>


### [170] [Real-Time Control-Constrained DDP for Underactuated Balancing of Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18552)
*SeongWon Nam,Hyunyong Lee,Hansol Kang,Jiman Park,Yeongwoo Son,Bumsu Yi,Jaeyoung Oh,Hyouk Ryeol Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于加速投影梯度的实时约束Differential Dynamic Programming（ABC-DDP）框架，专门针对欠驱动足式机器人的控制约束问题。该方法通过虚拟约束机制和可行驱动的多重射击框架，实现了从动态不可行初始状态的稳定优化，并支持实时模型预测控制（MPC）。仿真验证了四足机器人在统一MPC框架下的双足静态站立、慢速猫步、直立行走和高速奔跑等多种动态运动能力，首次实现了基于实时有限时域MPC的四足机器人静态双足站立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Differential Dynamic Programming（DDP）在处理控制约束方面存在局限性，难以高效求解约束优化问题。对于强欠驱动足式机器人而言，在保持实时性能的同时满足控制约束是一个关键挑战。此外，传统方法在处理动态不可行的初始状态时容易产生不稳定优化，限制了其在实际MPC应用中的可用性。因此，需要一种既能高效处理控制约束，又能保证实时性能的算法框架。

Method: 本文提出Accelerated Projected Gradient-based control-constrained DDP（ABC-DDP）方法，其核心创新包括：（1）基于加速投影梯度（APG）的约束求解器，可高效计算约束解并识别活跃集，避免了重复的Karush-Kuhn-Tucker（KKT）求逆操作；（2）引入虚拟约束机制，将控制约束集成到可行驱动的多重射击框架中；（3）采用短时域模型预测控制（MPC）架构，适应强欠驱动条件下的实时控制需求。

Result: 仿真结果展示了以下能力：（1）在外部扰动下的双足静态站立；（2）慢速猫步运动；（3）直立行走；（4）高速奔跑。所有动态运动均在统一的MPC框架下实现。实验验证了该方法在实时有限时域MPC控制下的有效性，首次实现了基于实时MPC的四足机器人静态双足站立。

Conclusion: 本文提出的ABC-DDP框架成功解决了欠驱动足式机器人的实时约束控制问题。通过结合加速投影梯度优化、虚拟约束机制和可行驱动的多重射击框架，该方法实现了高效稳定的约束优化，支持从动态不可行初始状态的收敛。仿真结果表明，该方法能够处理多种动态运动任务，包括首次在实时MPC框架下实现四足机器人双足静态站立，为欠驱动足式机器人的实际控制应用提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a real-time control-constrained Differential Dynamic Programming (DDP) framework for underactuated legged robots. To address the limitation of classical DDP in handling control constraints, we propose an Accelerated Projected Gradient (APG)-based control-constrained DDP (ABC-DDP), which efficiently computes constrained solutions and identifies active sets without repeated Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) inversions. A virtual constraint is introduced to integrate control constraints within a feasibility-driven multiple-shooting framework, enabling stable optimization even from dynamically infeasible initializations. The proposed method supports real-time model predictive control (MPC) with short horizons under strong underactuation. Simulation results demonstrate static two-leg standing under external disturbances, along with diverse dynamic motions including slow catwalk, upright walking, and high-speed running within a unified MPC framework. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of static two-leg standing of a quadruped robot achieved using real-time finite-horizon MPC.

</details>


### [171] [LabDex: A Hierarchical Benchmark for Dexterous Manipulation in Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18618)
*Zhipeng Tang,Sihang Chen,Sha Zhang,Peihao Yang,Yan Liu,Wentao Zhao,Xinrui Liu,Rui Huang,Wensheng Du,Yuting Huang,Jiajun Deng,Lidian Wang,Yuan Zhang,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LabDex是一个面向化学实验室的大规模灵巧操作数据集与基准，首次整合真实与仿真平台，通过原子技能-组合任务-长程实验的三级分层体系实现系统化评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作基准缺乏对实验室灵巧操作、真实环境交互及多阶段实验流程的综合评估能力，限制了自主实验室机器人的系统性训练与发展

Method: 构建跨平台分层任务分类体系：(1)统一真实/仿真环境标准化框架；(2)定义三级任务层级-原子技能(基础操作)/组合任务/长程工作流程；(3)提供多源演示数据与评估协议；(4)开展跨层级机器人学习方法验证实验

Result: 实验证明LabDex任务设计有效支持不同层级操作能力的系统评估，揭示基础技能与复杂实验流程的关联性，现有策略可在该基准上完成跨层级训练与性能分析

Conclusion: LabDex建立了首个覆盖完整实验室操作谱系的标准基准，为自主实验室机器人的研发提供可复现的评估框架和进化路径，推动领域从孤立技能向端到端实验能力迈进

Abstract: Autonomous laboratories hold great promise for accelerating scientific discovery. To achieve this vision, robots are supposed to dexterously manipulate diverse labware and instruments and execute long-horizon, state-dependent experimental procedures. Yet existing benchmarks do not jointly capture dexterous hand use, real-world laboratory interactions, and multi-stage experimental procedures, limiting systematic training and evaluation. To bridge this gap, we introduce LabDex, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark for dexterous manipulation in chemistry laboratories, organized around a hierarchical task taxonomy spanning atomic skills, compositional tasks, and long-horizon experiments. First, LabDex is cross-platform and, for the first time, unifies real-world and simulation platforms under a common framework, providing standardized task definitions, demonstrations, and evaluation protocols. Second, LabDex is large-scale and systematically organizes chemistry laboratory operations into three interconnected levels: Atomic Skills, which characterize fundamental dexterous manipulation capabilities; Compositional Skills; and Long-Horizon Laboratory Workflows. This hierarchical design not only supports the evaluation of end-task performance, but also enables the analysis of how fundamental dexterous skills compose and influence more complex laboratory operations. We conduct cross-level evaluations of representative robot learning methods in both real-world and simulation environments. The experimental results validate the effectiveness of the LabDex task design and demonstration data, and show that the benchmark supports the training and systematic evaluation of existing robotic policies across laboratory dexterous manipulation tasks at different levels, providing a foundation for further research and development of autonomous laboratory robots.

</details>


### [172] [Evaluation of Monocular SLAM Systems on High-Altitude Nadir UAV Footage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18632)
*Gašper Spagnolo,Matej Dobrevski,Danijel Skočaj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 对五种单目SLAM系统在航拍俯视视频上的基准测试表明，尽管存在回环检测能力，垂直定位和大范围轨迹仍严重失真，仅靠视觉的单目SLAM尚不足以实现可靠的航拍导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航拍俯视视频具有弱几何约束和严重感知混叠特性，对单目SLAM构成严峻挑战，需要系统评估现有方法在该场景下的性能。

Method: 在本地无人机飞行、合成城市级图像和长距离航拍序列三种数据上，对五种单目SLAM系统进行基准测试，不提供惯性或GNSS辅助以隔离视觉性能。

Result: 性能随环境和轨迹尺度差异显著。MASt3R-SLAM在DJI飞行序列上水平平均误差最低（参考路径长度的0.53%），但无系统能在长GES和ALTO序列上保持全局轨迹形状。DROID-SLAM综合表现最佳，平均误差为参考路径长度的2.88%。垂直定位仍较差，大范围轨迹即使有回环检测也严重失真。

Conclusion: 当前单目SLAM方法仅靠视觉信息尚不足以实现可靠的航拍导航，特别是在垂直定位和大范围轨迹方面存在显著缺陷。

Abstract: Aerial nadir video combines weak geometric constraints with severe perceptual aliasing, making it a difficult regime for monocular SLAM. We benchmark five monocular SLAM systems on local UAV flights, synthetic city-scale imagery, and long-range aerial sequences. To isolate visual performance, we provide no inertial or GNSS aiding. Performance varies strongly with environment and trajectory scale: MASt3R-SLAM achieves the lowest mean horizontal MAE on the five DJI flights (0.53\% of reference path length), whereas no system consistently preserves global trajectory shape on the long GES and ALTO sequences. Overall, DROID-SLAM performs best, averaging 2.88\% of reference path length across completed runs. Vertical position remains poor, and large-area trajectories remain highly distorted despite loop-closure capability. Current monocular SLAM methods are by themselves therefore insufficient for reliable visual-only aerial navigation.

</details>


### [173] [Progressive Experience Fusion for Multi-Task World Model Control in Endovascular Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18647)
*Harry Robertshaw,Maxence Boels,Nikola Fischer,Sebastien Ourselin,Christos Bergeles,Alejandro Granados,Thomas C Booth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出渐进经验融合（PEF）方法训练多任务TD-MPC2控制器，用于自主血管内导航，支持偏远地区机械取栓手术。实验显示PEF在多种血管解剖结构中显著提高成功率，并通过患者特定微调实现良好迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主血管内导航可将机械取栓技术扩展到服务不足地区，但控制器需能跨多变血管解剖结构导航长且多阶段路径。需要一种有效的多任务学习与患者自适应方法。

Method: 采用渐进经验融合（PEF）训练多任务TD-MPC2控制器。引入启发式方法，根据残差动作序列分散度调整模型预测路径积分（TD-MPC2）的规划horizon。在患者特定模拟环境中进行微调。实验包括五个子任务，在十个已知训练解剖结构上训练，在未见目标上测试。还测试了自适应horizon规划在30个血管结构上训练的控制器在十个未见血管结构上的表现。

Result: PEF在十个训练解剖结构上平均成功率74%，显著优于软演员-评论家（SAC）的37%（p<0.001）和基础TD-MPC2的65%（p=0.053）。自适应horizon规划的PEF控制器在30个血管结构上训练后，在十个未见血管结构上平均成功率90%。PEF智能体成功迁移至未见离体中风患者血管结构（在透视下），微调后平均路径比率从63%提升至80%（p<0.001），微调步骤为40x10^3（约相当于107分钟临床院间转运时间）。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了多血管训练和患者特定自适应的概念验证，为自主血管内导航在偏远地区的应用提供了可行方案，但仍需进一步验证才能临床部署。

Abstract: Autonomous endovascular navigation could support the delivery of mechanical thrombectomy to underserved areas, but controllers must navigate long, multi-stage paths across varying vascular anatomies. This study investigates Progressive Experience Fusion (PEF) to train a multi-task TD-MPC2 controller. We additionally evaluate a heuristic that changes the Model Predictive Path Integral planning horizon using residual action-sequence dispersion, and fine-tuning in a patient-specific simulation.
