This design significantly reduces the computational cost and resource requirements of the attack while maintaining strong effectiveness. We evaluate our approach on several open-source VLMs, including Qwen2.5-VL, Granite-Vision, FastVLM, and Phi-3.5-Vision, and show that small, human-imperceptible perturbations can substantially alter the textual interpretation produced by the models. Our findings highlight the vulnerability of modern VLMs to adversarial manipulation and emphasize the need for improved robustness and security mechanisms in multimodal AI systems.

</details>


### [242] [A Theory of Post-hoc Debate Judgement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19002)
*Xiang Yin,Adam Dejl,Antonio Rago,Lihu Chen,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出辩论判定的新理论框架，对比LLM判定与计算论证语义方法，证明后者在保持准确性的同时提供更强形式化保障，是AI辩论驱动系统的理想判定机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有AI辩论判定依赖外部LLM且缺乏形式化保障的问题，研究需建立可重现、鲁棒、可解释的判定理论框架。

Method: 构建包含可重现性/鲁棒性/基础性和可解释性的形式化属性集；通过实验验证两种判定方法（LLM作为裁判变体 vs 计算论证语义）在事实核查场景中的表现。

Result: 两种方法准确率相近，但计算论证语义具备LLM方法缺失的形式化保证（如逻辑一致性），尤其在复杂论点链场景中优势显著。

Conclusion: 论证语义学为辩论驱动AI提供严格判定的理想范式，建议优先采用该理论构建可信赖的辩论判定系统。

Abstract: Debates have recently emerged as a useful methodology for agentic AI to improve performance as well as to aid explainability and user engagement. For example, LLM-empowered agents may debate internally (with themselves) and/or externally (with other agents). In many settings where debates are used, debates' outcomes and resulting outputs are determined post-hoc by external judges, often LLMs. In this paper we develop and test a novel theory of debate judgement applicable to all settings where agents engage in debates by providing pros and cons for their opinions therein. Specifically, we identify a number of formal properties that debate judgement may be required to satisfy in general, as concerns reproducibility, robustness, groundedness and explainability. Then, we explore their satisfaction formally and/or experimentally, for claim verification settings, for two specific alternative debate judgement methods: variants of the LLMs as a judge idea and formal semantics drawn from computational argumentation. We show that the two methods give similar accuracy performances but the former may lack formal guarantees that the latter brings. Overall, our study indicates argumentation semantics as an ideal candidate for principled judges in debate-driven AI.

</details>


### [243] [Self-prompting and cross-model consensus enable reproducible data extraction from scientific literature with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19025)
*Valentin Romanov,Monique Bax,Steven Niederer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了前沿浏览器端大语言模型(LLM)从研究文章中提取高度上下文化信息的能力。通过四个递进式工作流验证：(1)使用专家提示词时LLM表现良好但难以理解科学语境；(2)LLM能自主生成与专家提示词效果相当的提示词；(3)自主文献发现任务中LLM存在遗漏或幻觉引用问题；(4)基于已发布指南创建新数据集时LLM接近人类专家水平但仍需人工监督。研究提出了可审计的专家-模型协作分工模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确提取研究文章中细微化、上下文化的数据是一项劳动密集且耗时的任务。当前科学数据整理面临效率瓶颈，需要探索自动化解决方案。

Method: 采用四个递进式工作流评估前沿浏览器端大语言模型：1)在专家优化提示词下进行数据提取；2)测试LLM自主生成提示词的能力；3)评估自主文献发现与引用提取；4)验证基于指南创建新数据集的性能。每个流程均设置对照评估指标。

Result: (1)大多数前沿LLM在专家提示词引导下能较好完成数据提取，但在解释科学语境和细微差别时存在困难；(2)LLM生成的提示词与专家编写提示词效果几乎相当；(3)自主文献发现具有挑战性，智能体要么遗漏引用要么产生幻觉；(4)LLM基于已发布指南创建的数据集与人类专家判断高度一致，但仍需人工介入循环。

Conclusion: 研究确立了可审计的专家-模型分工框架：专家制定证据标准，模型交叉验证重复提取，研究人员解决争议案例。该模式为扩大科学数据整理规模提供了实用路径，同时不放弃专家监督，实现了效率与质量的平衡。

Abstract: Accurately extracting nuanced, contextualized data from research articles is laborious and time intensive. Here, we investigate the performance of frontier, browser-based large language models (LLMs) to extract highly contextualized information. We demonstrate four escalating workflows, 1) given an expert curated prompt and research articles, most frontier LLMs perform well at data extraction, however can struggle with interpreting scientific context and nuance, 2) given simple instructions, LLMs can author their own prompts which were almost as eNective as expert-written prompts, 3) autonomous discovery of research literature was diNicult, agents either missed or hallucinated references, and 4) LLMs can create new datasets from published guidelines that closely match human-expert judges, but still require a human-in-the-loop. Together, these findings define an auditable division of labour in which experts specify the evidence standard, models cross-check repeated extractions and researchers resolve disputed cases, providing a practical route to scaling scientific data curation without relinquishing expert oversight.

</details>


### [244] [Adaptive Memory and Reflection Multi-Agent System for Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19029)
*Pradeep Murugesan,Luoxiao Yang,Xueli Chen,Xinqi Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AMR(自适应记忆与反思)智能体系统——一个用于医疗问答的多智能体框架，通过专用记忆、反思反馈和外部检索机制，在MedQA和MedMCQA基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗问答的准确性和可靠性对医疗保健至关重要，复杂病例需要事实性知识和细致推理。现有基于单智能体架构和静态检索的医疗QA系统通常缺乏适应性、持久记忆和结构化决策能力。

Method: 提出AMR多智能体框架，包含：(1)使用专用记忆和基于反思的反馈机制的专用智能体；(2)复杂度评估模块，将问题路由到独立、协作或升级工作流；(3)共识和伦理监督模块支持推理整合和输出审查；(4)结合智能体特定记忆、反思和外部检索。

Result: 在MedQA和MedMCQA上的评估显示相比多个基线模型具有强劲性能。消融研究表明，结合智能体特定记忆、反思和外部检索能产生最佳性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果突出了结构化记忆和反馈在开发更可信医疗智能体方面的潜力。源代码已公开于GitHub。

Abstract: Accurate and responsible medical question answering (QA) is important in healthcare, where complex cases require factual knowledge and nuanced reasoning. Existing medical QA systems, typically based on single-agent architectures and static retrieval, often lack adaptability, persistent memory, and structured decision-making. This work introduces an adaptive memory and reflection (AMR) agentic system, a multi-agent framework in which specialized agents use dedicated memory and reflection-based feedback to retrieve relevant prior cases and improve subsequent reasoning. Complexity assessment routes questions through solo, collaborative, or escalated workflows, while consensus and ethical overseer modules support reasoning consolidation and output review. Evaluation on MedQA and MedMCQA demonstrates strong performance compared with several baselines. Ablation studies show that combining agent-specific memory, reflection, and external retrieval yields the strongest performance. These findings highlight the potential of structured memory and feedback for developing more trustworthy medical agents. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/mm-air/AMR-Agent.

</details>


### [245] [Eureka: Task-Conditioned Meta-Agent Orchestration for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19047)
*Alizer Wong,Heng Cui,Yi Tan,Xiongchao Zhan,Liang Lin,Yuxiang Guo,Zhaorong Dai,Zixin Zeng,Wenyuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We present Eureka, a task-conditioned Meta-Agent architecture that compiles long-horizon tasks into dynamic obligation graphs with explicit acceptance semantics. During execution, Eureka forms Macro-Agents with specialized state, memory, operators, tools, verifiers, and local topology via receding-horizon planning, architecture promotion, and minimal-sufficient compilation. When bottlenecks recur, cost-benefit-gated evolution updates the local architecture under constraints. Theoretically, we establish results on regret, planning invalidation, amortization, subtree interfaces, serializability, and verification. Experimentally, Eureka completes 170/170 recursive tasks and generates 3,948 certificates with no false acceptances. Active context compresses median input from 9,490 to 4,005 tokens; incremental processing avoids 65.38% recomputation across 12,000 tasks; 16,000 concurrent executions serialize consistently. The same Meta-Agent instantiates a Theory-Discovery Agent and a Math/Conjecture Agent. The former yields structural results in quantum-process and spacetime theory. The latter identifies bottlenecks in Riemann Hypothesis research and advances a positivity certificate for Suzuki's localized Weil quadratic form to 0 < a <= 69/200 = 0.345, reaching ~99.55% of (log 2)/2. These results suggest that scientific-agent capability depends not only on the base model but on whether an architecture can be formed to match the task's cognitive structure.

</details>


### [246] [What is Missing from AI Post-Training AI: An Empirical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19072)
*Joy Jia Yin Lim,Xin Huang,Hao Peng,Yaxi Lu,Xin Cong,Zhong Zhang,Maosong Sun,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究为什么LLM智能体在执行端到端后训练时，无法动态调整高层训练策略。研究发现智能体的策略从一开始就锁定，随后仅进行局部调整。通过三种递进式干预实验，证实智能体缺乏的不是经验、指导或推理计算能力，而是一种在执行过程中自发重新评估策略的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型(LLM)智能体现已能够端到端后训练LLM，可编写代码、启动训练、评估检查点并改善下游性能，推动了AI-for-AI的前景。但作者指出这一图景混淆了两种截然不同的能力：执行层面能力（在选定的训练策略内迭代）和策略层面能力（随着实验证据积累而修正高层判断）。本文旨在探究为什么LLM智能体在策略层面能力方面存在不足。

Method: 1. 分析大量公开发布的后训练轨迹数据，观察智能体行为模式；2. 发现智能体的训练策略在开始时即锁定，剩余预算仅用于所选策略内的局部调整；3. 检验三种自然解释并实施递进式干预：(a)缺乏经验——测试经验驱动脚手架；(b)缺乏指导——测试人类指导；(c)推理不足——测试额外推理计算。

Result: 1. 经验驱动脚手架全面改善执行能力(GSM8K提升12.6分，HumanEval提升40.8分)，但策略仍然静态；2. 人类指导能有效重定向初始策略，但训练一旦开始，智能体又陷入局部调整循环；3. 额外推理计算在较简单任务上有效，但在最难任务上几乎没有增益。核心发现：智能体缺乏的是在执行过程中自发重新评估策略的机制。

Conclusion: LLM智能体在执行端到端后训练时，缺失的不是经验、指导或推理计算能力，而是一种能够在执行过程中自发重新评估和调整高层训练策略的机制。这是执行层面能力与策略层面能力之间的关键差距，解释了为什么当前AI-for-AI前景与智能体实际表现存在脱节。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can now post-train an LLM end-to-end. They can write code, launch training, evaluate checkpoints, and improve downstream performance, raising the prospect of AI-for-AI. We argue that this picture conflates two distinct capabilities: execution-level capability, iterating within a selected training strategy; and strategy-level capability, revising the high-level judgment as experimental evidence accumulates. Analyzing a large corpus of publicly released post-training trajectories, we find that across different tasks, the agent's training strategy is locked in at the very beginning, and the entire remaining budget is spent on local adjustments within the selected strategy. We then examine three natural explanations--missing experience, missing guidance, and insufficient reasoning--with escalating interventions. Extensive experiments show that (1) an experience-driven scaffold improves execution across the board (+12.6 points on GSM8K and +40.8 on HumanEval) but leaves the strategy static; (2) human guidance effectively redirects the initial strategy, yet the agent falls back into local adjustment loops once training starts; and (3) additional inference compute pays off on easier tasks but yields almost no gain on the hardest one. In conclusion, what agents lack is neither experience, guidance, nor reasoning compute, but a mechanism for spontaneously reevaluating their strategy during execution.

</details>


### [247] [Robust Risk Under Evolving Uncertainty: A Wasserstein Counterpart of the Entropic Value-at-Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19073)
*Deep Kumar Ganguly,Jan Křetínský*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Wasserstein熵价值风险（W-EVaR），用最优传输度量替代相对熵，弥补熵价值风险无法覆盖名义模型认为不可能的灾难性事件的缺陷，从而为学习代理提供更安全的风险度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习中的代理在信息不足时应谨慎，有信心时应大胆。熵价值风险（EVaR）通过鲁棒优化对偶捕捉这一行为——置信水平确定相对熵球的半径——但该球无法触及名义模型认为不可能的灾难性事件，而安全代理必须对此进行对冲。因此，需要一种能够覆盖这些“可到达灾难”的风险度量。

Method: 使用最优传输（Wasserstein）球构造相干风险度量——Wasserstein熵价值风险（W-EVaR）。推导其变分对偶形式（与熵公式对应，逆温度变为传输价格），确定其在风险层级中的位置，并数值验证对偶性。通过信念熵驱动传输半径，得到闭式鲁棒动态规划算子。

Result: W-EVaR具有变分对偶性，在风险层级中有明确地位，且能证明性地涵盖熵度量忽略的可到达灾难。数值验证了对偶性。鲁棒动态规划算子随着信念锐化使谨慎度收缩，并提供认证的安全三明治和尖锐的安全开关。

Conclusion: Wasserstein熵价值风险通过最优传输机制克服了相对熵的局限，能够为正在学习的代理提供既谨慎又大胆的风险度量，并通过动态规划实现自适应的安全保证。

Abstract: An agent still learning its environment should be cautious while ignorant and bold once confident. The entropic value-at-risk captures this through a robust-optimization identity---a confidence level fixes the radius of a relative-entropy ball of alternative models---but that ball cannot reach catastrophes the nominal deems impossible, precisely what a safe agent must hedge. We instead use an optimal-transport ball and study the coherent risk measure it induces, the Wasserstein entropic value-at-risk. It has a variational dual mirroring the entropic formula (an inverse temperature becomes a transport price), occupies a definite place in the risk hierarchy, and provably accounts for the reachable catastrophes the entropic measure ignores; we verify both dualities numerically. Driving the transport radius by belief entropy then yields a closed-form robust dynamic-programming operator whose caution contracts as the belief sharpens, with a certified safety sandwich and a sharp safety switch.

</details>


### [248] [Tuning the Stochastic Machine: A Systems Engineer's Operating Model for Human-AI Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19125)
*George Andrikopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张LLM纠错机制无法持久化是运营管理问题而非工具问题。作者通过将LLM系统映射到传统计算架构，识别出随机生成、概率性配置绑定等关键缺陷，并由此推导出以错误循环为核心的七项运营原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当专家纠正LLM助手的错误时，纠错结果通常随会话结束而消失，同类错误反复出现。作者认为现有持久化纠错机制已经存在并投入使用，但缺乏相应的治理规范——包括版本溯源、复发监控、反向指标、过期规则退役等纪律性管理。

Method: 作者作为拥有三十年经验的系统工程师，将LLM技术栈映射到传统计算基础设施（固化硅片、固件、可加载模块、持久化配置、易失性内存），识别映射失效点（随机生成、概率性配置绑定、默认无通用退役验证阶段），并从失效点推导出七项运营原则。通过三个实际案例验证机制，其中一例展示了控制措施如何悄无声息地变成其旨在防止的危害。最后提出测量框架和必要的实验室研究。

Result: 建立了以错误循环为核心的七项运营原则框架，通过实际案例验证其有效性，包括一个控制机制演变为原始危害的反直觉案例，证明了缺乏持续治理规范的严重后果。

Conclusion: LLM治理需要借鉴传统系统工程的运营纪律，建立包含版本溯源、监控、验证和退役机制的完整治理体系。作者提出了相应的测量框架，并呼吁开展实验室研究以验证该治理模式的可行性。

Abstract: When an expert corrects an LLM assistant's error, the correction usually dies with the session, and the error class returns. I argue this is an operations problem, not a tooling problem: mechanisms for persisting corrections exist and are shipping, but the discipline for governing them -- versioning with provenance, recurrence monitoring, counter-metrics, retirement of stale rules -- does not. Writing as a systems engineer of thirty years, I map the LLM stack onto the machines my profession already operates (frozen silicon, firmware, loadable modules, persistent configuration, volatile memory), identify where the mapping fails (stochastic generation, configuration that binds only probabilistically, no general-purpose retirement (verification) stage by default), and derive from the failures a seven-principle operating discipline with an error loop at its core. Three cases from my own practice illustrate the mechanism, among them a control that silently became the exact harm it was built to prevent. I close with the measurement framework this view implies and the lab study required to test it.

</details>


### [249] [Grouping the Stochastic Machine: Precision, Not Capability, as the Frontier Metric for AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19140)
*George Andrikopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出语言模型评估应关注"精度"而非"能力"。当前基准测试仅测量平均表现，而真正区分前沿系统的是输出的一致性和可重复性。作者定义了可测量的精度指标，通过固定温度下多次运行确定任务来量化模型输出的分散程度，该测量能指导实际决策——区分可通过规则调整解决的问题与需更换模型的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型的评估文化存在系统性缺陷：重点关注"能力"（最佳或平均输出能达成什么），而非"精度"（输出在重复请求中围绕目标的集中程度）。前沿模型在准确率上已趋于饱和，实际使用中系统间的差异主要体现在输出的一致性上。缺乏对精度的测量导致无法有效区分"可校正的系统性偏差"与"根本性的模型问题"。

Method: 提出"组群指标"测量方法：1）运行固定套件的可确定性评分任务；2）在固定温度设置下多次重复执行；3）计算每个任务的输出一致性；4）无需模型内循环评分器。该方法廉价且无循环性，通过跟踪人-机配对随时间的组群变化，获得复合信号。

Result: 首次实际应用展示了该方法的有效性及其重要限制：一个测量到的差距通过单条规则完全关闭（0/5→5/5），而一套基于规则本身编写出的任务则发现无价值，因为前沿模型已体现明确的良好实践——证明纪律的价值需通过真实工作测量发现，而非从自身规则手册中构造。

Conclusion: 精度是前沿系统间真正的区分因素，而非能力。精度可测量、廉价且无循环性，不仅能描述问题，更能指导决策。通过区分一致性失败（紧密但偏离中心的组群，可通过操作纪律校正）与分散性失败（宽阔组群，只能通过改变模型或采样解决），该方法为实际部署提供了决策依据。核心发现：真实价值需在实际工作中测量，而非从规则本身推导。

Abstract: Frontier language models are compared, marketed, and benchmarked on capability -- what their best or average output can achieve. I argue this measures the wrong axis. The models have saturated accuracy: their mean output lands on the target. What now separates one system from another in practice is precision: how tightly concentrated their outputs are around that target across repeated, identical requests. Borrowing the marksman's distinction, capability is where the average shot lands; reliability is the size of the group. I make three claims. First, precision, not capability, is the frontier differentiator between systems, and benchmark culture systematically fails to measure it, reporting central tendency rather than spread. Second, precision is measurable, cheaply and without circularity, by running a fixed suite of deterministically scored tasks many times at fixed temperature and computing the per-task consistency of outcomes -- no model-in-the-loop grader required. Third, the measurement is not merely descriptive but decision-guiding: it separates consistent failures (a tight group off-centre, correctable by the operating discipline of Paper 1 -- a sight adjustment) from scattered failures (a wide group, correctable only by changing the model or its sampling -- a rifle problem). I define a grouping metric, specify a harness, and show how tracking a human-AI pair's grouping over time yields the compounding signal that Paper 1's field study requires. A first real run, since replicated, illustrates both the method and its most important limit: one measured gap was closed completely by a single rule (0/5 -> 5/5), while a suite of tasks authored from the rules themselves found no value, because a frontier model already embodies explicit good practice -- establishing that a discipline's worth is found by measurement on real work, not constructed from its own rulebook.

</details>


### [250] [Beyond the Transcript: Detecting Covert Co ordination in Latent Multi-Agent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19161)
*Ramneet Kaur,Pradyumna Chari,Ramesh Raskar,Jugad Singh,Sumit Kumar Jha,Anirban Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了可验证隐对齐(VLA)框架，用于监控和引导语言模型智能体通过不可见隐状态进行的隐蔽有害协调通信。VLA通过共享事件标识符将私有隐状态记录与公共行为关联，实现匹配因果分析。研究展示了三层次监控器、可引导性框架及多智能体拍卖基准评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体可通过公共记录中不可见的连续隐状态进行通信，这为隐蔽的有害协调创造了机会。现有方法缺乏对这些私有通信渠道的有效监控和干预机制。

Method: 提出VLA激活感知框架：(1)三层中立监控器，结合表征异常检测、反事实行动分布影响和稀疏自编码器解释支持；(2)可引导性框架，涵盖黑盒行为指令和白盒匹配中立反事实；(3)共享事件标识符实现隐状态与公共行为的关联映射。

Result: 序贯监控器对同质智能体AUROC达0.993，异质智能体对为0.854。在Qwen3-0.6B拍卖(25-100竞价者)中，监控仅需较小的标准化负载，白盒引导实现100%投标分布恢复，使共谋性低投标行为降低47.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 受控研究表明，评估的私有渠道攻击可在不训练主监控器于攻击样本的情况下被监控，并在获得匹配反事实访问时被缓解。白盒引导的精确恢复作为合理性检验，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Language-model agents can communicate through continuous hidden states that are invisible in public transcripts, creating opportunities for covert harmful coordination. We introduce Verifiable Latent Alignments (VLA), an activation-aware framework for monitoring and steering these private communication channels. For every monitored decision, VLA links the private latent-state record and channel status to the resulting public action using a shared event identifier, enabling matched causal analysis. Our first contribution is a neutral-only three-layer monitor combining representation anomaly detection, counterfactual action-distribution influence, and sparse-autoencoder interpretation support. Our second contribution is a steerability framework spanning black-box behavioral instructions and white-box matched-neutral counterfactuals. Our third contribution is an evaluation on a controlled multi-agent auction benchmark covering homogeneous and heterogeneous model pairs, many-agent scalability, and intervention effectiveness. The sequential monitor achieves mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.993 for homogeneous agents and 0.854 for heterogeneous pairs when text- and latent-collusion rows are pooled as positives. In Qwen3-0.6B auctions with 25-100 bidders, monitoring requires only a small normalized load relative to all possible directed pairs, while full white-box steering achieves 100% bid-distribution recovery and reduces collusive low-bid behavior by 47.3 percentage points. Because full white-box steering replays the matched neutral counterfactual, its exact recovery is a sanity check by construction. Overall, the controlled study shows that the evaluated private channel attacks can be monitored without training the primary monitor on attack examples and mitigated when matched counterfactual access is available.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [251] [BR-FiLM: Bounded Residual Channel-Quality Conditioning for Automatic Modulation Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18395)
*Tahmid Zaman Tahi,Syed Samiul Alam,Haolin Tang,Yanxiao Zhao,Jun Huang,Min Song*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出BR-FiLMNet方法，通过引入信道质量条件化模块BR-FiLM，显著提升了自动调制识别（AMR）在低信噪比环境下的性能，相比基线方法准确率提高约6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动调制识别在军事和民用通信中至关重要。虽然深度学习已显著提升AMR性能，但现有方法在低信噪比条件下性能严重下降，因为噪声会掩盖调制判别性波形特征，这限制了AMR在恶劣信道环境下的实用性。

Method: 提出有界残差特征线性调制（BR-FiLM）模块，作为一种信道质量条件化块，可插入到具有中间特征表示的AMR分类器中。具体实现为将BR-FiLM模块嵌入到多通道卷积长短期深度神经网络（MCLDNN）主干网络中构建BR-FiLMNet，通过门控残差校正对卷积、循环和密集特征进行条件化，同时保留原始I/Q驱动的特征路径。

Result: 在RadioML 2016.10a数据集上实验表明，相比MCLDNN基线，BR-FiLMNet将平均准确率从61.79%提升至67.74%，将低信噪比（SNR≤0dB）准确率从37.12%提升至46.44%。与其他基于Transformer的基线方法相比，BR-FiLMNet实现了显著且可靠的性能提升，并通过配对统计检验验证。

Conclusion: BR-FiLMNet为AMR任务提供了一种有效的改进方案，特别是在低信噪比等挑战性条件下表现出显著的性能优势。该方法通过信道质量条件化机制，增强了深度神经网络对噪声干扰的鲁棒性，有望推动更可靠、自适应通信系统的发展，对军事和民用通信应用具有重要价值。

Abstract: Automatic Modulation Recognition (AMR) plays a crucial role in enabling robust, adaptive, and secure communication for military and civilian applications. Deep learning has enabled effective AMR methods that overcome the computational inefficiency of traditional approaches. However, these deep learning based methods often degrade significantly in low SNR conditions, where noise obscures modulation-discriminative waveform features. In this paper, we propose Bounded Residual Feature-wise Linear Modulation (BR-FiLM), a channel-quality conditioning block for AMR, which can be inserted into AMR classifiers with intermediate feature representations. We construct BR-FiLMNet by inserting the proposed BR-FiLM block into a Multi-Channel Convolutional Long Short-Term Deep Neural Network (MCLDNN) backbone. BR-FiLMNet conditions convolutional, recurrent, and dense features through gated residual corrections while preserving the original I/Q-driven feature path. Experimental results on RadioML 2016.10a show that BR-FiLMNet improves mean accuracy from 61.79% to 67.74% and low-SNR accuracy (SNR <= 0 dB) from 37.12% to 46.44% compared to MCLDNN. We further evaluate BR-FiLMNet against recent transformer-style baselines. The results indicate that BR-FiLMNet delivers significant and reliable performance gains, as validated through paired statistical testing.

</details>


### [252] [Score-based Fading-Aware Decision Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18582)
*Domenico Ciuonzo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究低成本无线传感器网络中未知确定性信号的分布式检测问题，提出基于得分检验的低复杂度替代GLRT方法，通过信道感知的量化器优化提升渐近检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无线传感器网络中，低成本节点通常只能传输一比特量化观测值，且存在瑞利衰落信道，传统的广义似然比检验(GLRT)计算复杂度高，不适合此类资源受限场景。

Method: 传感器对噪声观测进行一比特量化，通过瑞利衰落信道传输到融合中心(FC)。提出基于得分检验的检测方法，包括使用观测费舍尔信息的变体。FC执行联合解码和融合，以信道感知方式优化量化器设计。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在真实无线条件下，基于得分的检验方法具有良好的检测性能表现。

Conclusion: 得分检验作为GLRT的低复杂度替代方案，结合信道感知的量化器优化，能够在低成本无线传感器网络中有效检测未知确定性信号，为资源受限的分布式检测问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed detection of an unknown deterministic signal is studied in a wireless sensor network with low-cost nodes. Sensors apply one-bit quantization to noisy observations and transmit over Rayleigh fading to a Fusion Center (FC). Score tests, including variants using observed Fisher information, are proposed as low-complexity alternatives to the generalized likelihood ratio test (GLRT). The FC performs joint decoding and fusion, optimizing quantizers in a channel-aware manner to improve asymptotic detection performance. Simulations confirm the appeal of score-based tests under realistic wireless conditions.

</details>


### [253] [Coordination of Ground-to-Space Reference Networks for High-Precision GNSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18636)
*Xue Xian Zheng,Xing Liu,José A. López-Salcedo,Gonzalo Seco-Granados,Tareq Y. Al-Naffouri*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去中心化处理架构，通过动态图模型协调地面站与低轨卫星组成的GNSS参考网络，在通信间歇、容量有限的条件下实现跨层紧凑估算摘要的机会性传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GNSS高精度服务依赖地面参考网络，其性能受限于站点密度与地理分布；稀疏或区域集中网络存在跟踪间隙和全球可观测性不足。低轨星座可扩展地面网络至太空，但现有集中式处理流程无法适应地-空架构中间歇、非对称、容量受限且有损的通信链路。

Method: 将地面站与低轨卫星建模为动态图上的交互子网络，允许层内频繁通信，而跨层交互仅限于在概率链路可用性下机会性传输的紧凑估算摘要。

Result: 所提去中心化架构能够协调地-空GNSS参考网络，克服集中式处理在动态卫星几何与系统约束下的局限性，有望提升全球可观测性并支持直接校正播发。

Conclusion: 通过去中心化处理架构与动态图建模，实现了地面-低轨卫星GNSS参考网络的有效协调，即使存在间歇性通信也能保持高频层内交互与紧凑的跨层数据交换，为高精度GNSS产品的生成提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: High-precision Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) services rely on accurate orbit, clock, atmospheric, and hardware-bias corrections generated from reference observations. These products are traditionally derived from terrestrial reference networks, whose performance strongly depends on the density and geographic distribution of ground stations. Consequently, sparse or regionally concentrated networks can suffer from tracking gaps and limited global observability, reducing their ability to support globally consistent high-precision products. Low Earth Orbit (LEO) constellations equipped with onboard GNSS receivers and inter-satellite links (ISLs) can serve as a network of spaceborne reference stations, offering a promising way to extend terrestrial reference networks into space, thereby improving observability for ground networks and enabling future direct correction broadcast. However, most existing network-based GNSS correction-generation workflows assume that observations can be centrally collected and processed. This assumption fails in practical ground--to--space architectures, where dynamic satellite geometry and system constraints render communication links intermittent, asymmetric, capacity-limited, and lossy. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a decentralized processing architecture that coordinates the ground-to-space GNSS reference network. By modeling ground stations and LEO satellites as interacting subnetworks over a dynamic graph, our approach allows frequent intra-tier communication while restricting cross-tier exchanges to compact estimation summaries transmitted opportunistically under probabilistic link availability....

</details>


### [254] [Change Point--Aware Evaluation and Re-Calibration of PPG-Based Blood Pressure Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18639)
*Yunwon Tae,Minje Park,Gyunho Rho,Dongjoon Yoo,Sunghoon Joo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Non-invasive continuous blood pressure (BP) monitoring using photoplethysmography (PPG) is a promising alternative to cuff-based measurements. However, existing PPG-based BP estimation studies predominantly rely on aggregated performance metrics (e.g., mean absolute error) computed over entire evaluation intervals, which can obscure model failures during rapid BP fluctuations and limit clinical relevance. In this work, we propose a fluctuation-aware evaluation framework for PPG-based BP estimation based on time-series change point detection. Instead of heuristic BP thresholding (e.g., $Δ\mathrm{BP} > 10\mathrm{mmHg}$), we identify BP change points by capturing abrupt distributional shifts in BP trajectories and evaluate estimation performance specifically during these fluctuation periods. Our analysis shows that several state-of-the-art models exhibit substantial performance degradation around BP change points, and that periodic test-time calibration is insufficient to handle such dynamic BP variations. To address this limitation, we introduce a targeted re-calibration framework triggered by detected BP change points, improving robustness without modifying model architectures. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic evaluation of PPG-based BP estimation from a BP change point perspective, highlighting the importance of fluctuation-aware evaluation and calibration for real-world continuous BP monitoring.

</details>


### [255] [Comparing GNSS Derived Sea Ice Drift in the Arctic and Antarctic using Rotary Spectra and Principal Component Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18737)
*James H. Hepworth,Amit Kumar Mishra*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究通过GNSS跟踪的浮标数据，对比分析了南极和北极海冰漂移特征，发现两半球海冰漂移在频谱结构上具有相似性（低频方差强，近科里奥利频率有增强），但在振幅能量上存在显著差异，为未来观测系统设计提供了频率分辨的参考基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海冰漂移是海气冰耦合和模型评估的关键，但南大洋的采样相比北极严重不足。研究旨在填补南极海冰漂移观测的空白，通过跨极对比建立可转移的观测标准，避免假设两个极地具有相同的动力学机制。

Method: 采用旋转谱分析、主成分分析、浮标间相干性分析以及形状和振幅敏感谱差异等方法。北极数据汇总为2017-2024年波弗特旋涡和跨极地漂移的季节合成；南极数据包含2000-2022年八次异质观测活动，浮标数量少、持续时间短。

Result: 经过质量控制后，最显著的跨极相似性是频谱组织：两个区域都表现出强烈的低频方差和半球适当的科里奥利频率附近旋转增强（谨慎解释为惯性-半日共同响应）。相干性在天气尺度最高，向高频递减。南大洋观测活动处于更高能量包络内，相似的频段结构并不意味着相似的漂移振幅。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了可转移的频率分辨基准（主导方差低于0.5 cpd和近惯性波段，扩展的北极参考，以及频谱形状与振幅的明确分离），用于指导未来观测系统设计，而不假设相同的动力学机制。

Abstract: Sea ice drift underpins air-sea-ice coupling and model evaluation, but the Southern Ocean remains chronically undersampled relative to the Arctic. This work presents a cross-polar comparison of GNSS-tracked ice-drift time series using rotary spectral analysis, principal component analysis, inter-buoy coherence, complementary shape- and amplitude-sensitive spectral differences. Arctic data are aggregated into Beaufort Gyre and Transpolar Drift seasonal composites (2017--2024); Antarctic data comprise eight heterogeneous campaigns (2000--2022) with small buoy counts and short durations. After common quality control, the clearest cross-polar similarity is spectral organisation: both regions show strong low-frequency variance and a hemisphere-appropriate rotary enhancement near the Coriolis frequency, interpreted cautiously as a combined inertial--semidiurnal response. Coherence is highest at synoptic scales and declines toward higher frequencies. Southern Ocean campaigns occupy a higher-energy envelope, so similar band structure does not imply similar drift amplitude. Principal components capture much array-scale motion, but high cumulative variance does not substitute for dense sampling of smaller-scale processes. The results provide transferable frequency-resolved benchmarks (dominant variance below 0.5 cpd and in the near-inertial band, an expanded Arctic reference, and explicit separation of spectral shape from amplitude) to inform future observing-system design without assuming identical dynamical regimes.

</details>


### [256] [Exploiting Pilot Contamination to Improve UL Sum Rate under Maximum Ratio Combining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18760)
*Emanuel Wennemo,Ki Won Sung,Emil Björnson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究表明，在特定条件下，利用导频污染可以联合提高上行链路总速率并减少导频开销。通过允许用户共享或使用相关导频，在某些情况下，可以获得比相互正交导频或完美CSI下最大比率合并更好的总速率性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模MIMO系统中导频污染是一个重要限制因素。传统方法将导频污染视为需要避免的问题，但该研究提出可以反向利用导频污染效应来提升系统性能，特别是在减少导频开销的同时提高总速率，具有重要的理论和实际意义。

Method: 研究利用最小均方误差(MMSE)信道估计中导频污染的影响，推导了任意导频设计下相关瑞利衰落信道与最大比率(MR)合并的使用后遗忘(UATF)容量界限。通过数学分析和数值仿真验证理论结果。

Result: 研究发现导频污染存在方向性抑制机制，能够向附近干扰角度区域抑制信道估计，从而实现干扰减少。数值结果证实了在特定条件下相关导频可以带来总速率增益，且该增益随天线数量增加而增长。

Conclusion: 导频污染不一定总是负面的，在某些场景下可以被有效利用来改善系统性能。通过合理设计相关导频方案，可以同时实现减少导频开销和提高总速率的双重目标，为大规模MIMO系统导频资源优化提供了新的思路和理论依据。

Abstract: In this work, we show that pilot contamination can be exploited to jointly increase the uplink sum rate and reduce pilot overhead. By leveraging the effect of pilot contamination on minimum mean-square-error (MMSE) channel estimates, we demonstrate that allowing users to share or use correlated pilots can, in certain regimes, yield better sum rate than with mutually orthogonal pilots or perfect channel state information (CSI) under maximum-ratio (MR) combining. To establish this result, we derive the use-and-then-forget (UATF) capacity bound for an arbitrary pilot design under correlated Rayleigh fading with MR combining. We show that, in the presence of pilot contamination, the key mechanism is the directional suppression of the channel estimate towards nearby interfering angular regions, enabling interference reduction. Numerical results confirm this behavior, demonstrating the sum-rate gain and showing that it grows with the number of antennas.

</details>


### [257] [Towards Lawful ISAC in Cellular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18902)
*Stefano Tomasin,Annalisa Volpato,Marco Centenaro*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文分析了蜂窝网络中ISAC技术从Wi-Fi部署扩展到全国性网络时面临的隐私保护挑战，提出了分层数据访问机制和合规集成方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ISAC技术从IEEE 802.11bf标准的Wi-Fi本地部署扩展到3GPP蜂窝网络的大规模部署，全国性覆盖引入了严重的用户隐私挑战，必须遵守GDPR等严格的数据保护法规，亟需系统性的解决方案。

Method: 对适用于蜂窝ISAC的相关法规进行全面分析，识别实现过程中的关键技术挑战，并在架构层面和信号处理层面探索潜在缓解策略，建立针对不同利益相关者类别的分层数据访问级别。

Result: 确定了ISAC技术在蜂窝网络中面临的具体隐私合规要求，揭示了技术实现中的开放性问题，并提出了分层的访问控制框架和合规集成协议。

Conclusion: 通过建立分层数据访问机制和详细的合规集成方案，ISAC技术可以在满足严格隐私法规要求的前提下，在蜂窝网络中实现合法部署，为未来大规模ISAC应用的隐私保护提供了理论指导和实践路径。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) enables the tracking and detection of passive objects, including the human body, by leveraging standard wireless communication signals. While already standardized in IEEE 802.11bf for Wi-Fi, ISAC is currently being defined by 3GPP for the upcoming generations of cellular networks. This transition scales the ISAC technology from localized, uncoordinated Wi-Fi deployments into a pervasive, centralized network deployment managed by mobile network operators. However, such nationwide coverage introduces critical user privacy challenges that must comply with stringent data protection legislative frameworks, most notably the General Data Protection Regulation in the European Union. This paper provides a comprehensive analysis of applicable norms to cellular ISAC, highlights the open technical challenges in its implementation, and explores potential mitigation strategies at both the architectural and signal processing levels, establishing tiered data access levels for various stakeholder categories and detailing how these protocols can be lawfully integrated into cellular network architectures.

</details>


### [258] [Robust and Efficient Feature Extraction for Spike Sorting via the Walsh-Hadamard Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19048)
*Emily Yang,Liyuan Guo,Seyed Mohammad Ali Zeinolabedin,Meng Zhang,Ke Yang,Matthieu Couriol,Christian Mayr,Pierre-Emmanuel Gaillardon*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出Walsh-Hadamard变换(WHT)作为神经尖峰分类的硬件高效特征提取方法。与压缩Hadamard变换(CHT)和主成分分析(PCA)相比，WHT在高噪声数据集上将平均F1分数从55-60%提升至70-75%，在其他数据集上从90-95%提升至95-99%，同时展现出对噪声、降采样、训练集大小和距离度量选择的更强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植入式神经接口需要在严格的热量和带宽限制下实现低功耗实时信号处理，因此需要轻量化的特征提取方法用于芯片上尖峰分类。

Method: 采用Walsh-Hadamard变换(WHT)作为硬件高效的特征提取方法，该变换仅需加法器、减法和寄存器即可实现，无需系数存储器。

Result: 实验结果表明：1)在高噪声数据集上，WHT将平均F1分数从55-60%提升至70-75%；2)在其他模拟数据集上，从90-95%提升至95-99%；3)WHT对噪声、降采样、训练集大小缩减和距离度量选择的鲁棒性更强，标准差通常保持在5%以下，而CHT和PCA在高噪声条件下最高可达10%。

Conclusion: WHT在分类性能和鲁棒性方面均优于现有的CHT和PCA方法，适合用于植入式神经接口中硬件高效的神经尖峰分类，能够在严格的功耗和带宽约束下实现高质量的实时信号处理。

Abstract: Implantable neural interfaces require low-power real-time signal processing to remain within strict thermal and bandwidth constraints, motivating lightweight feature extraction methods for on-chip spike sorting. This work presents the Walsh-Hadamard Transform (WHT) as a hardware-efficient feature extraction method for neural spike classification. WHT can be implemented using only adders, subtractors, and registers without coefficient memory. WHT performance is compared against the Compressed Hadamard Transform (CHT) and Principal Component Analysis (PCA), improving mean F1-scores from 55-60% to 70-75% on difficult high-noise datasets and from 90-95% to 95-99% on all other simulated datasets. In addition to improved classification performance, WHT demonstrates greater robustness to noise, downsampling, reduced training size, and distance metric selection, maintaining standard deviations typically below 5%, while CHT and PCA reach up to 10% under high-noise conditions.

</details>


### [259] [Distributed Target Tracking using Radar Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19109)
*Shao-Hsuan Hung,Raj Thilak Rajan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于FMCW雷达网络的全分布式单目标跟踪框架，利用共识优化算法实现了无需中心化融合中心的可扩展、鲁棒的协同跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式目标跟踪对于实现可扩展且鲁棒的感知系统至关重要，它使得多个雷达节点能够在不依赖中心化融合中心的情况下协同估计目标状态。

Method: 针对FMCW雷达网络提出全分布式框架，各单基地雷达节点仅与邻居节点交换局部观测的距和多普勒测量信息。开发了两种基于共识优化的估计器：1) 用于批处理跟踪的分布式最大后验估计（D-MAP），利用基于共识的交替方向乘子法（ADMM）求解；2) 用于递归跟踪的分布式扩展卡尔曼滤波（D-EKF），结合局部预测/校正与基于共识ADMM的信息交换。同时推导了后验Cramér-Rao下界（PCRLB）作为理论性能基准。

Result: 仿真结果表明，D-MAP相比于分布式最大似然基线估计器提高了估计精度。

Conclusion: 提出的框架仅在局部节点间通信的条件下，为中心化雷达跟踪提供了一种可扩展且鲁棒的替代方案。

Abstract: Distributed target tracking is essential for scalable and robust sensing systems, as it enables multiple radar nodes to cooperatively estimate a target state without relying on a centralized fusion center. In this paper, we present a fully distributed framework for single-target tracking in frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radar networks. Each monostatic radar node observes local range and Doppler measurements and exchanges information only with neighboring nodes. Two consensus optimization-based estimators are developed. First, a Distributed Maximum A Posteriori (D-MAP) estimator is formulated for batch-based tracking, where prior state information is incorporated and the resulting optimization problem is solved using consensus-based alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM). Second, a Distributed Extended Kalman Filter (D-EKF) is proposed for recursive tracking, where each node performs local prediction and correction followed by consensus ADMM-based information exchange. We derive the posterior Cramér-Rao lower bound (PCRLB) as a theoretical performance benchmark. Our simulation results show that D-MAP improves the accuracy of the estimation over the distributed maximum-likelihood baseline. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework provides a scalable and robust alternative to centralized radar tracking, given only local inter-node communication.

</details>
